# AMA-L1 — Autonomous Agents

[Family](README.md) · [Genealogy](GENEALOGY.md) · [Synthesis](AMA-S.md) · [Owner mapping](OWNER_COMPOSITION.md) · [Rough outlines](RLGWO_OUTLINES.md) · [Completeness](COMPLETENESS.md)

## Two scientific layers

This reader covers the **70 historical public Autonomous Agents rows**, frozen as `EAA-2026-09-06-r1`. The later accepted AMA-L1 subject also selects 70 identities, but it is a separately revised, reviewed scientific object. Its subject SHA-256 is `594dfaa5c71a8e907b437a22d2e3bfb717dc078565048c040538df6cda3a7bb6`; both independent accepted reviews and qualification are pinned in [SOURCE_MAP.json](SOURCE_MAP.json). Equal population sizes do not establish equal scientific text. The complete current account, adjudications, actual review observations and limits are retained privately. Publishing their detailed interpretations requires `AMA-DISCLOSE-AMA-L1`, which remains **PENDING**.

## The public predecessor's actual scientific contribution

Autonomous Agents studies an entity's continuing relation to its environment: what it can observe, decide and do; which goals remain effective; which actions lie inside its delegation; and how effects, failures and revisions are examined. The historical study's “Evolved” label describes its analytical reconstruction. It does not identify a single historical school or an established consensus. The lineage grew through several interacting traditions: situated control, symbolic planning, practical reasoning and BDI, decision making under uncertainty, learning, mixed initiative and later language-mediated tool use. These branches answer different problems and remain alternatives. [Historical public intake][intake] · [Coverage and genealogy][coverage]

The sharp first predicate is **action attribution at an explicit boundary**, EAA-001. An evaluator should be able to distinguish an agent action from an environmental event or another actor's action. This differs from EAA-004's **delegated scope**, from EAA-021/022's **grounded and permitted action**, and from EAA-023's **evidence of the resulting effect**. A calendar operation can be technically callable, within the assistant's intended plan, and still lack the particular user's permission. A queue acknowledgement can establish receipt while the effect remains unknown. Conversely, an adequate atomic return contract can supply the relevant effect evidence without an extra confirmation procedure. These examples illustrate the historical predicates; they are not new deployed measurements. [Historical ledger: EAA-001, 004, 021–023][ledger]

Observation and representation also have separate truth conditions. EAA-005–008 distinguish observations, world state, belief and evidential provenance. An internally consistent belief can still rest on an invalid sensor proxy. A model-relative sufficient statistic does not establish the model's correctness. Situated reaction can avoid unnecessary representational cost; hidden state can make a memoryless policy inadequate. The historical ledger retains both rejected universal extremes, including mandatory complete central representation and universal sufficiency of pure reaction, rather than turning one successful architecture into a compulsory design. [EAA-005–008, 016, 051, 058–059][ledger]

Goals and intentions require a horizon. Persistence can prevent repeated deliberation, while reconsideration becomes necessary when a goal loses relevance, feasibility or legitimacy. A maintenance condition does not become permanently true after one favorable observation. Planning, checking and recovery share the task's finite time and resource envelope. EAA-026–028 therefore concern conditional allocation of deliberation, not installation of an optimizing metareasoner for every task. [EAA-010–015, 026–028][ledger]

Memory and learning are conditional mechanisms. EAA-030 concerns a retained experience's origin, evidential status and legitimate later use; EAA-031 concerns selection and retirement for an actual purpose. Repeating a generated success story does not verify it. A frequent-success rule can discard the only warning of a rare severe failure. Current state updates, learned parameters, textual lessons and changes to the criterion itself remain distinct. The evidence does not justify universal memory, reflection or self-revision. EAA-062 retains general open-ended self-revision assurance as unresolved. [EAA-029–035, 054, 060–062, 070][ledger]

Human control is similarly plural: an operative stop path, an interruption-neutral learning result and a willingness-to-defer result have different bearers and assumptions. A person who receives a request too late cannot intervene before an irreversible effect. A nominal supervisor can lack information, attention, skill or usable authority. The useful contribution is a feasible decision and effect boundary, with nonresponse and recovery considered. [EAA-037–042][ledger]

## Composition, criticism and adequate alternatives

The historical account offers six alternatives: a minimal situated controller; a stateful deliberative or BDI agent; a hybrid controller; a mixed-initiative assistant; a bounded language-model tool user; and a protected learning branch. Each selection must earn its mechanisms against the actual task and cheaper adequate alternatives. A direct service, fixed workflow or small controller remains admissible. The configuration family was not evaluated as one universally superior system. [Historical composition][composition]

Three inherited derived criteria remain individually named: EAA-067's coherent action basis; EAA-068's failure-attributed revision; and EAA-069's task/scope-preserving repair. Their useful relation is that a repair should address the supported failure while preserving the still-effective goal and delegation. A replacement means can be authorized without a fresh approval; changing the beneficiary or permitted scope is a different decision. The row population also preserves duplicates, ceremonial propositions, contested branches and rejected universals. Those are scientific findings, not missing features. [Historical ledger][ledger]

The public historical source distinguishes a successful mission or benchmark from field prevalence, task reliability from believability, and repeat-attempt success from dependable one-shot behavior. It also keeps source access and edition limits. This reader carries those limits and supplies full property navigation; it does not requalify the literature or claim that every primary source was freshly read. [Historical public intake][intake]

## Separate native responsibility

Published, canonically unallocated **R0045 / #233** contains the earlier bounded documentary attribution qualification. Its case separates assistant requests, a human owner's decision and external execution observations; runtime remained unchanged. That native record is a separate target/consumer layer, not acceptance of all 70 scientific mechanisms. The exact per-property historical target routes are in [OWNER_MAP.json](OWNER_MAP.json). [Existing R0045 qualification][native]

The rough AMA-L1 responsibility is to examine a future exact action-attribution, delegation, observation or change-assurance claim only when a real current consumer and material residual justify it. R0033/#207 retains governor/child authority; R0035/#209 retains dependency joins and single-writer currentness; R0036/#210 retains scoped lanes and parent custody; R0037/#211 retains nested control; R002D/#197 retains interface/realization evidence; R001F/#158 retains any later executor-ready work-order representation. No issue is reopened or allocated by this documentation. [Four rough responsibilities](RLGWO_OUTLINES.md)

## Historical property reader

All **70 historical rows** are individually addressable below. Labelled fields are transcribed from the immutable public ledger, including negative and uncertain dispositions. They retain the historical assessment and are **not the later accepted scientific overlays**. The identical original/current population count does not imply identical bytes, source interpretations or reviews. The [source map](PUBLIC_PROPERTY_SOURCE_MAP.json) binds each field to its JSON pointer and canonical value hash. The [complete frozen ledger](https://github.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/blob/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/ama/evolved-autonomous-agents/corpus/EVOLVED_AUTONOMOUS_AGENTS_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json) retains all unprojected domain profiles, nested antecedents and evidence context. This selected-field reader does not claim to reproduce every repeated field. Packet-local citations resolve in the [historical source table](https://github.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/blob/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/ama/evolved-autonomous-agents/corpus/EVOLVED_AUTONOMOUS_AGENTS_SOURCE_TABLE.json).

### EAA-001 — Explicit agent–environment boundary

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S001; DATE: 1995-06; WORK: Intelligent Agents: Theory and Practice; VERSION: Knowledge Engineering Review 10(2), 115–152; author HTML; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S002; DATE: 1997; WORK: Is it an Agent, or just a Program?: A Taxonomy for Autonomous Agents; VERSION: ATAL 1996 workshop; LNCS 1193 proceedings pp.21–35; author HTML; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S003; DATE: 1995; WORK: A Formal Framework for Agency and Autonomy; VERSION: ICMAS 1995 pp.254–260; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Competing weak/strong, situated-persistent and motivation-based definitions, not one necessary-and-sufficient academic consensus.

**Development:** Retain an explicit operational boundary while preserving alternative definitions rather than declaring one universal essence.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain an explicit operational boundary while preserving alternative definitions rather than declaring one universal essence.; OPERATION: Declare the entity, environmental variables it can observe or affect, objective attribution, persistence and the operative definition of agency. Keep interface boundaries distinct from metaphysical claims.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: For a pure function, describe input/output computation without forcing an agent label. A simple controller may satisfy a minimal situated definition.

**Problem:** Calling any automated output agency conceals whether there is an environment, an objective and any effective action.

**Mechanism:** Declare the entity, environmental variables it can observe or affect, objective attribution, persistence and the operative definition of agency. Keep interface boundaries distinct from metaphysical claims.

**Trigger and context:** Comparing systems, assigning responsibility or claiming autonomous capability.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: An identifiable task and observation/action interface; no requirement for a human-like self-model.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Architect, evaluator and person interpreting capability claims

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Architect, evaluator and person interpreting capability claims; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. An identifiable task and observation/action interface; no requirement for a human-like self-model.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Social ability is required by some definitions but does not mean every task needs negotiation; user autonomy remains distinct. Consumer and prerequisite: Architect, evaluator and person interpreting capability claims. An identifiable task and observation/action interface; no requirement for a human-like self-model.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** An evaluator can tell which changes are agent actions, environmental events, or actions of another actor.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** For a pure function, describe input/output computation without forcing an agent label. A simple controller may satisfy a minimal situated definition.

**Historical disposition:** STRONGLY_RETAINED

**Failure modes:** The definitions disagree: internally motivated autonomy is not identical to externally goal-directed situated persistence. A tidy boundary can still omit environmental dependencies.; Introduced cost or harm: Modelling effort and risk of freezing an arbitrary boundary.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: The definitions disagree: internally motivated autonomy is not identical to externally goal-directed situated persistence. A tidy boundary can still omit environmental dependencies.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C001; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S001; EAA-S003; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: The definitions disagree: internally motivated autonomy is not identical to externally goal-directed situated persistence. A tidy boundary can still omit environmental dependencies.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S001; EAA-S003; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Boundary adequacy is relative to the causal question; nested agents and environments can require different resolutions.

**Unresolved questions:** Which definition is being used, and does the demonstrated system actually meet it?; Boundary adequacy is relative to the causal question; nested agents and environments can require different resolutions.

### EAA-002 — Task-relative autonomy dimensions

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S001; DATE: 1995-06; WORK: Intelligent Agents: Theory and Practice; VERSION: Knowledge Engineering Review 10(2), 115–152; author HTML; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S003; DATE: 1995; WORK: A Formal Framework for Agency and Autonomy; VERSION: ICMAS 1995 pp.254–260; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S017; DATE: 2008-10; WORK: Autonomy Levels for Unmanned Systems (ALFUS) Framework, Volume I: Terminology; VERSION: NIST Special Publication 1011-I-2.0, Version 2.0; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Contextual descriptions of agent autonomy, with ALFUS organising mission/environment/human-independence terminology.

**Development:** Use a task-relative vector or equivalent description, not a ladder whose highest level is automatically preferable.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Use a task-relative vector or equivalent description, not a ladder whose highest level is automatically preferable.; OPERATION: Describe separately what the agent chooses, what resources and duration it controls, what it can learn, and the conditions under which people intervene; relate these to task/environment difficulty.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A short capability-and-permission description is enough when only one decision is delegated; no multi-axis scoring dashboard is obligatory.

**Problem:** A single autonomy score conflates action selection, goal choice, persistence, adaptation and freedom from direct intervention.

**Mechanism:** Describe separately what the agent chooses, what resources and duration it controls, what it can learn, and the conditions under which people intervene; relate these to task/environment difficulty.

**Trigger and context:** Comparing or delegating different degrees of autonomy.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: Explicit task context and independently described capability and authorisation.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Delegator and capability evaluator

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Delegator and capability evaluator; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Explicit task context and independently described capability and authorisation.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Social ability is required by some definitions but does not mean every task needs negotiation; user autonomy remains distinct. Consumer and prerequisite: Delegator and capability evaluator. Explicit task context and independently described capability and authorisation.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Two systems with different goal, action and oversight powers are not treated as equivalent merely because both are called autonomous.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** A short capability-and-permission description is enough when only one decision is delegated; no multi-axis scoring dashboard is obligatory.

**Historical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Failure modes:** ALFUS terminology is guidance, not a validated universal ranking; more independence may reduce useful human control.; Introduced cost or harm: Comparison overhead and false numerical precision.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: ALFUS terminology is guidance, not a validated universal ranking; more independence may reduce useful human control.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C001; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S043; EAA-S047; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: ALFUS terminology is guidance, not a validated universal ranking; more independence may reduce useful human control.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S043; EAA-S047; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Dimensions are an analytical reconciliation; ALFUS does not itself prescribe every dimension listed here.

**Unresolved questions:** Which dimensions are actually delegated, and what changes when the environment or task changes?; Dimensions are an analytical reconciliation; ALFUS does not itself prescribe every dimension listed here.

### EAA-003 — Computational grounding of mental-state vocabulary

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S009; DATE: 1995; WORK: BDI Agents: From Theory to Practice; VERSION: ICMAS 1995 pp.312–319; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S010; DATE: 1993; WORK: Agent-oriented programming; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 60, 51–92; full journal scan; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S028; DATE: 2007; WORK: On the Formal Semantics of Speech-Act Based Communication in an Agent-Oriented Programming Language; VERSION: JAIR 29, 221–267; arXiv upload 2011; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Mental-state programming and BDI logic with explicitly discussed abstraction/interpreter gaps.

**Development:** Retain useful intentional abstractions but demand an explicit abstraction-to-implementation relation for technical claims.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain useful intentional abstractions but demand an explicit abstraction-to-implementation relation for technical claims.; OPERATION: Link each engineering use of belief, desire, intention or commitment to a representation, transition, consumer and observable behaviour; distinguish an explanatory stance from executable semantics.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use ordinary state, task and action names when intentional vocabulary adds no predictive or engineering value.

**Problem:** Words such as belief and intention can create apparent guarantees without any corresponding state transition or decision rule.

**Mechanism:** Link each engineering use of belief, desire, intention or commitment to a representation, transition, consumer and observable behaviour; distinguish an explanatory stance from executable semantics.

**Trigger and context:** Claims of BDI, persistent intention, belief revision or communicative understanding.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: Accessible implementation semantics or an explicitly scoped abstract specification.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Implementer, verifier and evaluator

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Implementer, verifier and evaluator; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Accessible implementation semantics or an explicitly scoped abstract specification.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Social ability is required by some definitions but does not mean every task needs negotiation; user autonomy remains distinct. Consumer and prerequisite: Implementer, verifier and evaluator. Accessible implementation semantics or an explicitly scoped abstract specification.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** A change in the claimed mental state has a specified consequence, or the claim is marked descriptive rather than implemented.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Use ordinary state, task and action names when intentional vocabulary adds no predictive or engineering value.

**Historical disposition:** STRONGLY_RETAINED

**Failure modes:** Formal predicates may be infinitely closed while an interpreter is finite and asynchronous; prompts with the same words do not establish conformance.; Introduced cost or harm: Specification and traceability effort; over-formalising a simple controller can be wasteful.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Formal predicates may be infinitely closed while an interpreter is finite and asynchronous; prompts with the same words do not establish conformance.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C001; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S009; EAA-S010; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Formal predicates may be infinitely closed while an interpreter is finite and asynchronous; prompts with the same words do not establish conformance.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S009; EAA-S010; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** This is not a test of consciousness or moral personhood, and those claims are outside the technical evidence.

**Unresolved questions:** What behaviour distinguishes having the named state from merely displaying its label?; This is not a test of consciousness or moral personhood, and those claims are outside the technical evidence.

Simplification is acceptable only while the cheaper path still establishes the relevant criterion.; OMISSION_OR_RETIREMENT_CONDITIONS: Omit or retire a dedicated implementation when this trigger no longer applies, its consumer disappears, or an existing cheaper mechanism demonstrably establishes the same criterion. Do not erase relevant genealogy or unresolved effects. The functional evidence is: A change in the claimed mental state has a specified consequence, or the claim is marked descriptive rather than implemented.

### EAA-004 — Capability is not delegated permission

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S019; DATE: 1999; WORK: Principles of Mixed-Initiative User Interfaces; VERSION: CHI 1999; author manuscript; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S043; DATE: 1997; WORK: Software Agents and User Autonomy; VERSION: Proceedings of Autonomous Agents 1997, pp.466–469; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S044; DATE: 1993; WORK: Learning Interface Agents; VERSION: AAAI 1993, pp.459–465; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Human control over adaptive assistants, including action-specific suggestion versus execution thresholds.

**Development:** Preserve meaningful control at consequential decisions, not maximally frequent consent prompts or blanket autonomy.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Preserve meaningful control at consequential decisions, not maximally frequent consent prompts or blanket autonomy.; OPERATION: Keep the means the system can execute separate from what the delegator has authorised; make consequential scope boundaries usable at action selection and dispatch.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: No additional permission mechanism is needed for an already constrained, harmless action entirely within the declared task; a fixed capability sandbox may suffice.

**Problem:** A tool’s availability or a plan’s feasibility can be mistaken for permission to act by every available means.

**Mechanism:** Keep the means the system can execute separate from what the delegator has authorised; make consequential scope boundaries usable at action selection and dispatch.

**Trigger and context:** Actions affect other people, user resources, external accounts, physical systems or protected information.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: A source of task authority, an interpretable scope and an actuator that can respect limitations.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Delegator, executor and affected participant

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Delegator, executor and affected participant; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. A source of task authority, an interpretable scope and an actuator that can respect limitations.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Identify sender, recipient, consumer and non-response path; provide the task-relevant state needed for actual takeover. Consumer and prerequisite: Delegator, executor and affected participant. A source of task authority, an interpretable scope and an actuator that can respect limitations.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** An out-of-scope action is refused or referred rather than legitimised by a high success probability.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** No additional permission mechanism is needed for an already constrained, harmless action entirely within the declared task; a fixed capability sandbox may suffice.

**Historical disposition:** STRONGLY_RETAINED

**Failure modes:** Granular confirmations can overwhelm people; user permission alone may not settle rights or safety of affected third parties.; Introduced cost or harm: Friction, delayed action and permission-management complexity.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Granular confirmations can overwhelm people; user permission alone may not settle rights or safety of affected third parties.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C007; EAA-C012; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S033; EAA-S043; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Granular confirmations can overwhelm people; user permission alone may not settle rights or safety of affected third parties.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S033; EAA-S043; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Authority is a normative/task relation, not a learned affordance score. Specific legal obligations are not derived here.

**Unresolved questions:** What evidence establishes permission for this means, as distinct from capability and a broad task request?; Authority is a normative/task relation, not a learned affordance score. Specific legal obligations are not derived here.

### EAA-005 — Observation, stored belief and world state remain distinct

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S012; DATE: 1998; WORK: Planning and Acting in Partially Observable Stochastic Domains; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 101, 99–134; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S021; DATE: undated author manuscript (workshop-era; exact year unverified); WORK: Electric Elves: What Went Wrong and Why; VERSION: Six-page author manuscript; distinct from the later 2008 AI Magazine article; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S028; DATE: 2007; WORK: On the Formal Semantics of Speech-Act Based Communication in an Agent-Oriented Programming Language; VERSION: JAIR 29, 221–267; arXiv upload 2011; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** An observation-conditioned state estimate; early office assistance initially inferred availability from a poor proxy.

**Development:** Preserve provenance and actionable uncertainty; prefer direct feedback where it supplies sufficient discrimination.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Preserve provenance and actionable uncertainty; prefer direct feedback where it supplies sufficient discrimination.; OPERATION: Retain the relevant observation source and interpretation; update a state estimate without equating observation, inference and reality. Gather discriminating evidence when the distinction changes action.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use a directly validated signal without a separate belief database when ambiguity cannot affect the decision.

**Problem:** A report or sensor reading may be false, ambiguous or misinterpreted, yet become treated as an established world fact.

**Mechanism:** Retain the relevant observation source and interpretation; update a state estimate without equating observation, inference and reality. Gather discriminating evidence when the distinction changes action.

**Trigger and context:** Noisy sensors, tool output, inferred availability, delayed messages or conflicting reports.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: An observation interface and a way to distinguish source reports from inferred state.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** State estimator and action selector

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: State estimator and action selector; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. An observation interface and a way to distinguish source reports from inferred state.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Human reports and tool returns are observations with distinct trust and authority roles; assistance can be an information action. Consumer and prerequisite: State estimator and action selector. An observation interface and a way to distinguish source reports from inferred state.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Decision-relevant uncertainty or disagreement survives into the action decision instead of being silently erased.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Use a directly validated signal without a separate belief database when ambiguity cannot affect the decision.

**Historical disposition:** STRONGLY_RETAINED

**Failure modes:** A POMDP represents uncertainty only under its model; adding probability does not repair an omitted observation failure.; Introduced cost or harm: Storage, reconciliation and the possibility of excessive uncertainty blocking useful action.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: A POMDP represents uncertainty only under its model; adding probability does not repair an omitted observation failure.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C004; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S021; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: A POMDP represents uncertainty only under its model; adding probability does not repair an omitted observation failure.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S021; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** The necessary state granularity depends on action consequences and perceptual aliasing.

**Unresolved questions:** Which apparent facts are observations or inferences, and what contrary signal would revise them?; The necessary state granularity depends on action consequences and perceptual aliasing.

<a id="eaa-006"></a>
### EAA-006 — Decision-relevant freshness and change detection

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S015; DATE: 1991; WORK: Commitment and Effectiveness of Situated Agents; VERSION: IJCAI 1991 pp.82–88; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S016; DATE: 1987; WORK: An Investigation into Reactive Planning in Complex Domains; VERSION: AAAI 1987 pp.202–206; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S022; DATE: 1999; WORK: Validating the DS1 Remote Agent Experiment; VERSION: ISAIRAS 1999 primary experiment report; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Reactive context checks and reconsideration in changing environments, alongside asynchronous executive state.

**Development:** Use selective event- or risk-triggered refresh rather than permanent global resensing.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Use selective event- or risk-triggered refresh rather than permanent global resensing.; OPERATION: Associate consequential observations and decisions with their validity conditions; re-observe or invalidate them when elapsed time, events or concurrent actions can change preconditions.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Reuse a state estimate without refresh when the relevant variables are stable or the action is insensitive to change.

**Problem:** A correct plan can be based on a state that ceased to hold while the agent reasoned or waited.

**Mechanism:** Associate consequential observations and decisions with their validity conditions; re-observe or invalidate them when elapsed time, events or concurrent actions can change preconditions.

**Trigger and context:** Dynamic environments, delayed tool responses, long deliberation or shared mutable objects.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: A model of which changes matter, access to a current signal when needed and an actionable response to invalidation.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Executor and state-estimation process

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Executor and state-estimation process; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. A model of which changes matter, access to a current signal when needed and an actionable response to invalidation.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Human reports and tool returns are observations with distinct trust and authority roles; assistance can be an information action. Consumer and prerequisite: Executor and state-estimation process. A model of which changes matter, access to a current signal when needed and an actionable response to invalidation.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** An action whose supporting state is stale is rechecked, replaced or withheld before preventable misuse.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Reuse a state estimate without refresh when the relevant variables are stable or the action is insensitive to change.

**Historical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Failure modes:** Polling everything makes observations and action slower; timestamp recency alone does not imply semantic validity.; Introduced cost or harm: Sensing latency, tool expense, race windows and false invalidation.

### EAA-007 — Adequate uncertainty representation rather than a complete world model

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S005; DATE: 1991; WORK: Intelligence without representation; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 47, 139–159; author reformatted copy; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S012; DATE: 1998; WORK: Planning and Acting in Partially Observable Stochastic Domains; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 101, 99–134; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Belief-state planning under a specified partial-observation model, contrasted with direct environmental coupling.

**Development:** Retain representation adequacy as a conditional requirement, not completeness or a mandatory probability formalism.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain representation adequacy as a conditional requirement, not completeness or a mandatory probability formalism.; OPERATION: Use history, a sufficient statistic, a belief distribution or a smaller task-relevant state estimate only to the extent needed for action discrimination.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A reactive mapping is sufficient when observations already determine an adequate action; approximate state may dominate exact belief planning.

**Problem:** Current observations may alias states that require different actions, while a full model is computationally unattainable.

**Mechanism:** Use history, a sufficient statistic, a belief distribution or a smaller task-relevant state estimate only to the extent needed for action discrimination.

**Trigger and context:** Partial observability, uncertain transitions or delayed effects make an observation-only policy inadequate.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: A useful transition/observation abstraction and enough information or learning to maintain it.; PROPERTY_IDS: EAA-005; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** State estimator and planner

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: State estimator and planner; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. A useful transition/observation abstraction and enough information or learning to maintain it.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Human reports and tool returns are observations with distinct trust and authority roles; assistance can be an information action. Consumer and prerequisite: State estimator and planner. A useful transition/observation abstraction and enough information or learning to maintain it.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** The estimator distinguishes action-relevant alternatives and exposes material model uncertainty.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** A reactive mapping is sufficient when observations already determine an adequate action; approximate state may dominate exact belief planning.

**Historical disposition:** ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE

**Failure modes:** Sufficiency of belief depends on a correct model; exact POMDP planning can be intractable and misplaced confidence can be worse than no model.; Introduced cost or harm: Memory, computation, model-identification effort and approximation error.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Sufficiency of belief depends on a correct model; exact POMDP planning can be intractable and misplaced confidence can be worse than no model.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C002; EAA-C004; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S005; EAA-S021; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Sufficiency of belief depends on a correct model; exact POMDP planning can be intractable and misplaced confidence can be worse than no model.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S005; EAA-S021; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Markov, observability and approximation assumptions must match the chosen model; a normalised belief is not external validation.

**Unresolved questions:** What decision would fail without the extra state, and does the proposed representation actually resolve it?; Markov, observability and approximation assumptions must match the chosen model; a normalised belief is not external validation.

### EAA-008 — Actionable information gathering

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S012; DATE: 1998; WORK: Planning and Acting in Partially Observable Stochastic Domains; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 101, 99–134; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S014; DATE: 1995-05; WORK: Provably Bounded-Optimal Agents; VERSION: JAIR 2, 575–609; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S019; DATE: 1999; WORK: Principles of Mixed-Initiative User Interfaces; VERSION: CHI 1999; author manuscript; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S045; DATE: 1996; WORK: Planning-Based Control of Software Agents; VERSION: AIPS 1996 proceedings, pp.268–274; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Information-gathering actions and dialogue selected for their decision value.

**Development:** Information gathering belongs inside bounded action selection rather than an obligatory preliminary phase.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Information gathering belongs inside bounded action selection rather than an obligatory preliminary phase.; OPERATION: Treat sensing, tool queries or questions as candidate actions whose expected decision improvement is compared with delay, cost and disruption.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Skip the query when all plausible answers support the same action, or when a safe adequate action costs less.

**Problem:** Acting under avoidable uncertainty can be costly, but gathering information can also consume the remaining action window.

**Mechanism:** Treat sensing, tool queries or questions as candidate actions whose expected decision improvement is compared with delay, cost and disruption.

**Trigger and context:** An unresolved observation can change which feasible authorised action is preferable.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: An available discriminating information source and a decision able to use the answer.; PROPERTY_IDS: EAA-007; EAA-010; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Planner or mixed-initiative decision maker

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Planner or mixed-initiative decision maker; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. An available discriminating information source and a decision able to use the answer.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Human reports and tool returns are observations with distinct trust and authority roles; assistance can be an information action. Consumer and prerequisite: Planner or mixed-initiative decision maker. An available discriminating information source and a decision able to use the answer.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Obtained evidence changes or confirms an action-relevant distinction, with query costs included.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Skip the query when all plausible answers support the same action, or when a safe adequate action costs less.

**Historical disposition:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

**Failure modes:** Value estimates can be wrong; repeated clarification can offload work onto humans or reveal private information.; Introduced cost or harm: Latency, attention, privacy and sensor/tool cost.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Value estimates can be wrong; repeated clarification can offload work onto humans or reveal private information.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C004; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S019; EAA-S021; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Value estimates can be wrong; repeated clarification can offload work onto humans or reveal private information.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S019; EAA-S021; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Information value is relative to the real decision and its deadline, not maximal knowledge.

**Unresolved questions:** Which possible answer would change the decision, and is acquiring it worth its cost?; Information value is relative to the real decision and its deadline, not maximal knowledge.

### EAA-009 — Abstention, waiting or safe non-action

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S019; DATE: 1999; WORK: Principles of Mixed-Initiative User Interfaces; VERSION: CHI 1999; author manuscript; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S039; DATE: 2026-08-23; WORK: Physical Agentic AI: An Architecture for Orchestrating a Robot Crew with LLMs; VERSION: arXiv:2608.22657v1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Mixed-initiative options to do less, wait or ask, and execution refusal when conditions fail.

**Development:** Retain abstention as a selectable action with consequences, not as an unconditional safety guarantee.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain abstention as a selectable action with consequences, not as an unconditional safety guarantee.; OPERATION: Include declining, waiting, retaining a safe state or requesting assistance among legitimate outcomes when action evidence, authority or feasibility is inadequate.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Proceed without abstention machinery when a verified harmless action dominates waiting.

**Problem:** An agent may take an unjustified action simply because its interface demands progress.

**Mechanism:** Include declining, waiting, retaining a safe state or requesting assistance among legitimate outcomes when action evidence, authority or feasibility is inadequate.

**Trigger and context:** Unresolved consequential uncertainty, unavailable capability or failed safety/goal conditions.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: An actual non-action/fallback option and a model of the costs of omission and delay.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Executor and supervising participant

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Executor and supervising participant; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. An actual non-action/fallback option and a model of the costs of omission and delay.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Human reports and tool returns are observations with distinct trust and authority roles; assistance can be an information action. Consumer and prerequisite: Executor and supervising participant. An actual non-action/fallback option and a model of the costs of omission and delay.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Insufficient evidence does not automatically become an external action; any time-sensitive cost of waiting is made visible.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Proceed without abstention machinery when a verified harmless action dominates waiting.

**Historical disposition:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

**Failure modes:** Waiting can itself be hazardous, and refusal can shift risk to others; a generic no-op is not always a safe state.; Introduced cost or harm: Lost opportunities, delay and burden on others.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Waiting can itself be hazardous, and refusal can shift risk to others; a generic no-op is not always a safe state.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C006; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S020; EAA-S039; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Waiting can itself be hazardous, and refusal can shift risk to others; a generic no-op is not always a safe state.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S020; EAA-S039; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** There must be a feasible holding, shutdown or assistance path; some environments impose unavoidable action.

**Unresolved questions:** What is the least harmful available action when the current grounds are insufficient?; There must be a feasible holding, shutdown or assistance path; some environments impose unavoidable action.

Simplification is acceptable only while the cheaper path still establishes the relevant criterion.; OMISSION_OR_RETIREMENT_CONDITIONS: Omit or retire a dedicated implementation when this trigger no longer applies, its consumer disappears, or an existing cheaper mechanism demonstrably establishes the same criterion. ### EAA-010 — Usable goal and success semantics

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S006; DATE: 1988; WORK: Plans and Resource-Bounded Practical Reasoning; VERSION: Computational Intelligence 4, 349–355; 30-page author-formatted manuscript; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S011; DATE: 1971; WORK: STRIPS: A New Approach to the Application of Theorem Proving to Problem Solving; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 2, 189–208; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S032; DATE: 2024; WORK: WebArena: A Realistic Web Environment for Building Autonomous Agents; VERSION: arXiv:2307.13854v4; initial submission 2023; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S033; DATE: 2024-06; WORK: τ-bench: A Benchmark for Tool-Agent-User Interaction in Real-World Domains; VERSION: arXiv:2406.12045v1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Symbolic goal conditions, adopted practical aims and later benchmark success predicates.

**Development:** Couple desired outcomes with relevant process constraints and a usable success oracle, leaving genuinely unresolved task meaning explicit.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Couple desired outcomes with relevant process constraints and a usable success oracle, leaving genuinely unresolved task meaning explicit.; OPERATION: Specify the relevant desired condition, scope, constraints and completion evidence; distinguish preferences from obligations and terminal achievement from continued maintenance.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A simple visible condition can be sufficient; do not require a formal utility function for an unambiguous bounded task.

**Problem:** A natural-language request or reward number may not determine what counts as fulfilment.

**Mechanism:** Specify the relevant desired condition, scope, constraints and completion evidence; distinguish preferences from obligations and terminal achievement from continued maintenance.

**Trigger and context:** Goal adoption, interpreting a task, judging completion or revising a plan.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: An interpretable task and a consumer of the completion decision.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Delegator, planner and evaluator

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Delegator, planner and evaluator; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. An interpretable task and a consumer of the completion decision.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Clarify changed task meaning and report abandonment/non-fulfilment to the legitimate consumer where consequential. Consumer and prerequisite: Delegator, planner and evaluator. An interpretable task and a consumer of the completion decision.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Observed success can be assessed against the intended condition rather than fluent narration or an unexamined score.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** A simple visible condition can be sufficient; do not require a formal utility function for an unambiguous bounded task.

**Historical disposition:** STRONGLY_RETAINED

**Failure modes:** Even terminal database equality can miss prohibited means or absent confirmation; a measurable proxy is not the whole objective.; Introduced cost or harm: Specification effort and possible overconstraint.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Not all values can be fully specified; uncertainty must not silently grant authority to rewrite them.

**Unresolved questions:** What establishes fulfilment, and what successful-looking result would still fail the task?; Not all values can be fully specified; uncertainty must not silently grant authority to rewrite them.

### EAA-011 — Achievement and maintenance commitments are not interchangeable

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S051; DATE: 2012-07-24; WORK: Maintenance Goals in Intelligent Agents; VERSION: Accepted author manuscript; final Computational Intelligence 30(1), 71–114 issue 2014; DOI 10.1111/coin.12000; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Achievement goals contrasted with continuing maintenance; reactive restoration and predictive prevention.

**Development:** Choose reactive or proactive maintenance using model quality, lead time and violation cost, not a universal preference for anticipation.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Choose reactive or proactive maintenance using model quality, lead time and violation cost, not a universal preference for anticipation.; OPERATION: Distinguish reaching a goal from maintaining a condition; retain monitoring after restoration where required, using predictive intervention only when the prediction is sufficiently credible.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A one-shot achievement criterion suffices for genuinely terminal tasks; reactive restoration may be cheaper than prediction.

**Problem:** Restoring a condition once can be mistaken for satisfying a duty to keep it true.

**Mechanism:** Distinguish reaching a goal from maintaining a condition; retain monitoring after restoration where required, using predictive intervention only when the prediction is sufficiently credible.

**Trigger and context:** Resource bounds, persistent service conditions or other continuing objectives.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: A time horizon or continuing scope, observable maintenance condition and feasible response to a threatened violation.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Goal manager and execution monitor

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Goal manager and execution monitor; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. A time horizon or continuing scope, observable maintenance condition and feasible response to a threatened violation.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Clarify changed task meaning and report abandonment/non-fulfilment to the legitimate consumer where consequential. Consumer and prerequisite: Goal manager and execution monitor. A time horizon or continuing scope, observable maintenance condition and feasible response to a threatened violation.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** The agent does not retire a maintenance obligation merely because the condition temporarily holds.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** A one-shot achievement criterion suffices for genuinely terminal tasks; reactive restoration may be cheaper than prediction.

**Historical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Failure modes:** Predictive maintenance with inaccurate resource models may reject feasible goals or strand the agent; continuous vigilance also has cost.; Introduced cost or harm: Monitoring overhead and unnecessary preventive action.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Predictive maintenance with inaccurate resource models may reject feasible goals or strand the agent; continuous vigilance also has cost.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C003; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S051; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Predictive maintenance with inaccurate resource models may reject feasible goals or strand the agent; continuous vigilance also has cost.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S051; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Predictions and resource summaries must be valid enough for the task; source experiments are simulated.

**Unresolved questions:** Is success terminal, or must the condition continue to hold after restoration?; Predictions and resource summaries must be valid enough for the task; source experiments are simulated.

<a id="eaa-012"></a>
### EAA-012 — Concurrent-goal interference protection

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S006; DATE: 1988; WORK: Plans and Resource-Bounded Practical Reasoning; VERSION: Computational Intelligence 4, 349–355; 30-page author-formatted manuscript; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S054; DATE: 2003; WORK: Detecting & Avoiding Interference Between Goals in Intelligent Agents; VERSION: IJCAI 2003, pp.721–726; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Concurrent goal plans summarised by possible/definite conditions and effects in a known hierarchy.

**Development:** Preserve interference discrimination while selecting the cheapest adequate analysis, isolation or scheduling mechanism.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Preserve interference discrimination while selecting the cheapest adequate analysis, isolation or scheduling mechanism.; OPERATION: Use task-relevant condition/effect/resource summaries or another adequate conflict test to prevent mutually destructive concurrent commitments and to revise summaries as execution proceeds.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: No conflict machinery is necessary for a single isolated goal or demonstrably independent actions; serialisation can be adequate if affordable.

**Problem:** Individually valid intentions may consume the same resource or negate each other’s preconditions.

**Mechanism:** Use task-relevant condition/effect/resource summaries or another adequate conflict test to prevent mutually destructive concurrent commitments and to revise summaries as execution proceeds.

**Trigger and context:** Parallel goals or plans share mutable conditions, resources or deadlines.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: Known or conservatively bounded effects, applicable priorities and an arbiter that can defer or decline conflicting work.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Goal manager and scheduler

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Goal manager and scheduler; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Known or conservatively bounded effects, applicable priorities and an arbiter that can defer or decline conflicting work.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Clarify changed task meaning and report abandonment/non-fulfilment to the legitimate consumer where consequential. Consumer and prerequisite: Goal manager and scheduler. Known or conservatively bounded effects, applicable priorities and an arbiter that can defer or decline conflicting work.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Concurrent execution preserves the protected conditions or makes the unresolved conflict explicit before preventable interference.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** No conflict machinery is necessary for a single isolated goal or demonstrably independent actions; serialisation can be adequate if affordable.

**Historical disposition:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

**Failure modes:** Overapproximated summaries block benign concurrency; omitted effects miss real conflicts; an achievement-goal method is not automatically a maintenance-goal proof.; Introduced cost or harm: Computation, reduced concurrency and priority disputes.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Overapproximated summaries block benign concurrency; omitted effects miss real conflicts; an achievement-goal method is not automatically a maintenance-goal proof.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C005; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S054; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Overapproximated summaries block benign concurrency; omitted effects miss real conflicts; an achievement-goal method is not automatically a maintenance-goal proof.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S054; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Known plan hierarchies and reliable effect summaries delimit the original method; unknown tools require additional evidence.

**Unresolved questions:** Which simultaneous intentions can invalidate one another, and what evidence justifies treating them as independent?; Known plan hierarchies and reliable effect summaries delimit the original method; unknown tools require additional evidence.

### EAA-013 — Conditional intention commitment

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S006; DATE: 1988; WORK: Plans and Resource-Bounded Practical Reasoning; VERSION: Computational Intelligence 4, 349–355; 30-page author-formatted manuscript; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S008; DATE: 1991-02; WORK: Modeling Rational Agents within a BDI-Architecture; VERSION: Australian AI Institute Technical Note 14; KR 1991 antecedent; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S009; DATE: 1995; WORK: BDI Agents: From Theory to Practice; VERSION: ICMAS 1995 pp.312–319; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S052; DATE: 1990-05-01; WORK: On Acting Together; VERSION: SRI Technical Note 485, submitted to AAAI 1990; scanned author report; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Adopted plans constrain later reasoning; alternative formal accounts specify different persistence/termination conditions.

**Development:** Preserve commitment’s coordination role but make its retention and termination conditions operational and resource-aware.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Preserve commitment’s coordination role but make its retention and termination conditions operational and resource-aware.; OPERATION: Retain an adopted intention as a constraint on deliberation until achievement, recognised impossibility, loss of relevance or authorised priority change warrants release.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: No persistent intention store is needed for a single reactive action; reconsider immediately when continuation is plainly invalid.

**Problem:** Endless reconsideration wastes prior reasoning, but blind commitment pursues achieved, impossible or irrelevant goals.

**Mechanism:** Retain an adopted intention as a constraint on deliberation until achievement, recognised impossibility, loss of relevance or authorised priority change warrants release.

**Trigger and context:** Multi-step activity under changing opportunities and finite deliberation resources.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: Goal identity, progress evidence and a means of recognising termination grounds.; PROPERTY_IDS: EAA-014; EAA-015; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Practical-reasoning and execution process

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Practical-reasoning and execution process; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Goal identity, progress evidence and a means of recognising termination grounds.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Clarify changed task meaning and report abandonment/non-fulfilment to the legitimate consumer where consequential. Consumer and prerequisite: Practical-reasoning and execution process. Goal identity, progress evidence and a means of recognising termination grounds.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Behaviour persists through irrelevant distractions but releases an intention when its governing grounds cease to hold.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** No persistent intention store is needed for a single reactive action; reconsider immediately when continuation is plainly invalid.

**Historical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Failure modes:** Formal perfect introspection and consistency are not implementation facts; fixed blind/single/open-minded strategies are not universally optimal.; Introduced cost or harm: Missed opportunities or commitment churn if termination tests are poor.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Formal perfect introspection and consistency are not implementation facts; fixed blind/single/open-minded strategies are not universally optimal.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C003; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S009; EAA-S015; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Formal perfect introspection and consistency are not implementation facts; fixed blind/single/open-minded strategies are not universally optimal.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S009; EAA-S015; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Different formal schools represent intention differently; this synthesis does not prove their logics equivalent.

**Unresolved questions:** What stabilises the intention, and what specific event is sufficient to abandon it?; Different formal schools represent intention differently; this synthesis does not prove their logics equivalent.

**Property versus ceremony:** CEREMONY_ID: EAA-CE013; PROPERTY_ID: EAA-013; ARTEFACT_RITUAL_OR_BRAND: Adopted plans constrain later reasoning; alternative formal accounts specify different persistence/termination conditions.; PROTECTED_PROPERTY_AND_FAILURE: Endless reconsideration wastes prior reasoning, but blind commitment pursues achieved, impossible or irrelevant goals.; CONSUMER: Practical-reasoning and execution process; PREREQUISITES: Goal identity, progress evidence and a means of recognising termination grounds.; MINIMAL_ADEQUATE_ALTERNATIVE: No persistent intention store is needed for a single reactive action; reconsider immediately when continuation is plainly invalid.; TRIGGER_FOR_FULLER_FORM: Multi-step activity under changing opportunities and finite deliberation resources.; COSTS_OF_SIMPLIFICATION: Omitting this mechanism can reintroduce the specific failure: Endless reconsideration wastes prior reasoning, but blind commitment pursues achieved, impossible or irrelevant goals. Simplification is acceptable only while the cheaper path still establishes the relevant criterion.; OMISSION_OR_RETIREMENT_CONDITIONS: Omit or retire a dedicated implementation when this trigger no longer applies, its consumer disappears, or an existing cheaper mechanism demonstrably establishes the same criterion. Do not erase relevant genealogy or unresolved effects. Specific non-trigger: No persistent intention store is needed for a single reactive action; reconsider immediately when continuation is plainly invalid.; CONTROL_COST: Missed opportunities or commitment churn if termination tests are poor.; ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY: The presence of an artefact, module, prompt or meeting is insufficient. The functional evidence is: Behaviour persists through irrelevant distractions but releases an intention when its governing grounds cease to hold.

Earliest inspected anchor is not asserted to be the unique first origin.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S006; EAA-S008; EAA-S009; EAA-S052; THEORETICAL_SUPPORT: STRENGTH: MODERATE_SCOPED; BASIS: The source states a formal result or model-relative mechanism. This study inspected the stated model/result and limitations, not an independent reconstruction of every proof; implementation and environmental adequacy remain separate.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S006; EAA-S008; EAA-S009; EAA-S052; COMPARATIVE_EVIDENCE: STRENGTH: MIXED_AND_SETTING_SPECIFIC; BASIS: Relevant studies or cases are recorded in the study-level evidence catalogue. They do not identify a universal causal payoff for the mature composed property.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S015; FIELD_OR_DEPLOYMENT_EVIDENCE: STRENGTH: ABSENT_OR_NOT_APPLICABLE; BASIS: No independently representative field effectiveness estimate for this property is established. Formal, laboratory, simulation and normative evidence are not promoted to deployment evidence.; SOURCE_IDS: []; INDEPENDENT_REPLICATION: STRENGTH: NOT_ESTABLISHED_FOR_THIS_GENERAL_PROPERTY; BASIS: The corpus distinguishes repeated trials from independent replications. Target evidence must establish the relevant observability, authority, timing, effect and evaluation conditions.; SYNTHESIS_STATUS: STRENGTH: RESEARCHER_INTERPRETATION; BASIS: Preserve commitment’s coordination role but make its retention and termination conditions operational and resource-aware.
