### EAA-044 — Action-faithful explanation and calibrated reliance

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S044; DATE: 1993; WORK: Learning Interface Agents; VERSION: AAAI 1993, pp.459–465; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S043; DATE: 1997; WORK: Software Agents and User Autonomy; VERSION: Proceedings of Autonomous Agents 1997, pp.466–469; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S047; DATE: 2026-05-03; WORK: Why Johnny Can’t Use Agents: Industry Aspirations vs. User Realities with AI Agents; VERSION: arXiv:2509.14528v2; initial submission September 2025; CAIS 2026 accepted metadata; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Case-based explanations of learned assistance, challenged when they obscure actual criteria.

**Development:** Retain recipient- and action-specific explanatory usefulness, not compulsory chain-of-thought narration or anthropomorphic persuasion.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain recipient- and action-specific explanatory usefulness, not compulsory chain-of-thought narration or anthropomorphic persuasion.; OPERATION: Explain the action-relevant evidence, limits, uncertainty and available correction at the decision point; assess whether the recipient can use it, not merely whether a rationale is fluent.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Visible direct feedback or a simple status display may be more useful than a long explanation; no rationale is needed for an inconsequential self-evident action.

**Problem:** A plausible explanation or human-like persona can make people rely on behaviour they cannot actually predict or control.

**Mechanism:** Explain the action-relevant evidence, limits, uncertainty and available correction at the decision point; assess whether the recipient can use it, not merely whether a rationale is fluent.

**Trigger and context:** People must approve, correct, understand or take over a consequential decision.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: A known recipient/decision and access to facts genuinely related to the action process.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** User, reviewer or takeover recipient

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: User, reviewer or takeover recipient; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. A known recipient/decision and access to facts genuinely related to the action process.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Identify sender, recipient, consumer and non-response path; provide the task-relevant state needed for actual takeover. Consumer and prerequisite: User, reviewer or takeover recipient. A known recipient/decision and access to facts genuinely related to the action process.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** The explanation enables appropriate reliance or correction without implying unestablished competence or certainty.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Visible direct feedback or a simple status display may be more useful than a long explanation; no rationale is needed for an inconsequential self-evident action.

**Historical disposition:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

**Failure modes:** A nearest-example story can misrepresent the real decision criteria; think-aloud usability findings do not establish a universal explanation format.; Introduced cost or harm: Attention, disclosure of sensitive information and false reassurance.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: A nearest-example story can misrepresent the real decision criteria; think-aloud usability findings do not establish a universal explanation format.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C007; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S043; EAA-S047; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: A nearest-example story can misrepresent the real decision criteria; think-aloud usability findings do not establish a universal explanation format.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S043; EAA-S047; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Faithfulness and comprehension require separate evidence; revealing internal text does not by itself establish either.

**Unresolved questions:** What decision can the recipient make better with this explanation, and is it faithful to what drove the action?; Faithfulness and comprehension require separate evidence; revealing internal text does not by itself establish either.

### EAA-045 — Adequate independent outcome and policy oracle

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S032; DATE: 2024; WORK: WebArena: A Realistic Web Environment for Building Autonomous Agents; VERSION: arXiv:2307.13854v4; initial submission 2023; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S033; DATE: 2024-06; WORK: τ-bench: A Benchmark for Tool-Agent-User Interaction in Real-World Domains; VERSION: arXiv:2406.12045v1; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S031; DATE: 2023-10-10; WORK: Reflexion: Language Agents with Verbal Reinforcement Learning; VERSION: arXiv:2303.11366v4; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Functional task state tests, repeated-trial reward definitions and internally generated code tests.

**Development:** Retain claim-adequate evaluation and oracle criticism rather than mandatory model-on-model judging or a single reward.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain claim-adequate evaluation and oracle criticism rather than mandatory model-on-model judging or a single reward.; OPERATION: Choose observations and tests adequate to the claimed outcome and protected process constraints; separate generated assertions from evaluation evidence, using independent checks where needed.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A trustworthy direct return contract or simple external predicate may suffice; a separate evaluator model is not inherently better.

**Problem:** The same mechanism that generates an answer can certify its own error, and a terminal-state score can omit prohibited actions.

**Mechanism:** Choose observations and tests adequate to the claimed outcome and protected process constraints; separate generated assertions from evaluation evidence, using independent checks where needed.

**Trigger and context:** Performance claims, learning feedback, completion certification or safety claims.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: An explicit target, observable effects/process evidence and a test that can reject plausible but incorrect outputs.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Evaluator, learning-feedback consumer and completion reporter

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Evaluator, learning-feedback consumer and completion reporter; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. An explicit target, observable effects/process evidence and a test that can reject plausible but incorrect outputs.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Disclose user simulation, human assistance and affected-party costs; protected tests must not externalise harm. Consumer and prerequisite: Evaluator, learning-feedback consumer and completion reporter. An explicit target, observable effects/process evidence and a test that can reject plausible but incorrect outputs.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Success is judged against the intended claim, with uncovered policy or effect dimensions explicitly identified.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** A trustworthy direct return contract or simple external predicate may suffice; a separate evaluator model is not inherently better.

**Historical disposition:** STRONGLY_RETAINED

**Failure modes:** Oracles can be incomplete or wrong; even an independent judge may share blind spots, and terminal database equality can miss absent consent.; Introduced cost or harm: Evaluation expense, false positives/negatives and incomplete observability.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Oracles can be incomplete or wrong; even an independent judge may share blind spots, and terminal database equality can miss absent consent.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C011; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S031; EAA-S033; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Oracles can be incomplete or wrong; even an independent judge may share blind spots, and terminal database equality can miss absent consent.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S031; EAA-S033; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Independence is functional/evidential, not guaranteed by using a different prompt or agent name.

**Unresolved questions:** What wrong result or prohibited trajectory could this oracle still mark successful?; Independence is functional/evidential, not guaranteed by using a different prompt or agent name.

### EAA-046 — Repeated trials, versions and reproducible conditions

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S033; DATE: 2024-06; WORK: τ-bench: A Benchmark for Tool-Agent-User Interaction in Real-World Domains; VERSION: arXiv:2406.12045v1; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S035; DATE: 2024-07-01; WORK: AI Agents That Matter; VERSION: arXiv:2407.01502v1; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S032; DATE: 2024; WORK: WebArena: A Realistic Web Environment for Building Autonomous Agents; VERSION: arXiv:2307.13854v4; initial submission 2023; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Repeated evaluations with different success aggregations and disclosed model/task configurations.

**Development:** Retain reproducible measurement and dependence accounting, not one mandatory seed count or aggregate metric.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain reproducible measurement and dependence accounting, not one mandatory seed count or aggregate metric.; OPERATION: Report task units, attempts, resets, seeds/model/tool versions and aggregation; distinguish any-success sampling from success on every repeated trial, and preserve enough configuration for meaningful reproduction.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A deterministic bounded mechanism may need a smaller condition-coverage test rather than arbitrary repeated identical runs.

**Problem:** A single successful trajectory or best-of-many result can hide instability and cannot identify a dependable ongoing service.

**Mechanism:** Report task units, attempts, resets, seeds/model/tool versions and aggregation; distinguish any-success sampling from success on every repeated trial, and preserve enough configuration for meaningful reproduction.

**Trigger and context:** Stochastic agents, adaptive environments, comparative evaluation or reliability claims.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: Defined trial units, controlled/reset state where appropriate and access to relevant configuration.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Evaluator and consumer of reliability claims

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Evaluator and consumer of reliability claims; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Defined trial units, controlled/reset state where appropriate and access to relevant configuration.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Disclose user simulation, human assistance and affected-party costs; protected tests must not externalise harm. Consumer and prerequisite: Evaluator and consumer of reliability claims. Defined trial units, controlled/reset state where appropriate and access to relevant configuration.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Results reveal what was repeated and how failures count; repeated descriptions of one case are not called replication.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** A deterministic bounded mechanism may need a smaller condition-coverage test rather than arbitrary repeated identical runs.

**Historical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Failure modes:** Runs may share prompts, task templates, datasets, infrastructure and human assistance; a statistical interval does not remove model or selection bias.; Introduced cost or harm: Test expense, configuration preservation and practical limits on closed-model reproducibility.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Runs may share prompts, task templates, datasets, infrastructure and human assistance; a statistical interval does not remove model or selection bias.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C011; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S035; EAA-S036; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Runs may share prompts, task templates, datasets, infrastructure and human assistance; a statistical interval does not remove model or selection bias.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S035; EAA-S036; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Trial independence and stationarity must be assessed; resets can remove the very accumulation that matters in deployment.

**Unresolved questions:** What is the unit of success, what was repeated, and which conditions would another evaluator need to reproduce it?; Trial independence and stationarity must be assessed; resets can remove the very accumulation that matters in deployment.

Simplification is acceptable only while the cheaper path still establishes the relevant criterion.; OMISSION_OR_RETIREMENT_CONDITIONS: Omit or retire a dedicated implementation when this trigger no longer applies, its consumer disappears, or an existing cheaper mechanism demonstrably establishes the same criterion. Do not erase relevant genealogy or unresolved effects. ### EAA-047 — Resource-controlled comparison with credible baselines

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S035; DATE: 2024-07-01; WORK: AI Agents That Matter; VERSION: arXiv:2407.01502v1; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S036; DATE: 2024-10-29; WORK: Agentless: Demystifying LLM-based Software Engineering Agents; VERSION: arXiv:2407.01489v2; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Re-evaluation of accuracy together with inference/retry cost and constrained non-agent baselines.

**Development:** Retain fair frontier comparisons rather than assuming either agentic or non-agentic methods should win.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain fair frontier comparisons rather than assuming either agentic or non-agentic methods should win.; OPERATION: Compare methods at meaningful cost/quality points with shared task access and honest budgets; include a competitive fixed workflow, retry policy or non-agent alternative when applicable.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: For a feasibility demonstration, label it as such rather than fabricating a comparative claim; do not demand a full trial when no superiority claim is made.

**Problem:** An architectural improvement can be confounded with more tokens, retries, information or favourable task selection.

**Mechanism:** Compare methods at meaningful cost/quality points with shared task access and honest budgets; include a competitive fixed workflow, retry policy or non-agent alternative when applicable.

**Trigger and context:** Claims that agentic freedom, reflection or a module improves results.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: Comparable task sets, resource accounting and disclosed differences in information or tuning.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Comparable task sets, resource accounting and disclosed differences in information or tuning.

Consumer and prerequisite: Experimenter and architecture decision maker. Comparable task sets, resource accounting and disclosed differences in information or tuning.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** An observed gain is not attributed to architecture while ignoring materially different resources or oracle access.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** For a feasibility demonstration, label it as such rather than fabricating a comparative claim; do not demand a full trial when no superiority claim is made.

**Historical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Failure modes:** Equal tokens need not equal user value; one baseline may favour a task family and a stronger architecture may earn its cost elsewhere.; Introduced cost or harm: Benchmarking and tuning effort; oversimplified budget matching can itself distort comparison.

### EAA-048 — Whole-task cost and burden accounting

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S014; DATE: 1995-05; WORK: Provably Bounded-Optimal Agents; VERSION: JAIR 2, 575–609; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S019; DATE: 1999; WORK: Principles of Mixed-Initiative User Interfaces; VERSION: CHI 1999; author manuscript; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S035; DATE: 2024-07-01; WORK: AI Agents That Matter; VERSION: arXiv:2407.01502v1; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S021; DATE: undated author manuscript (workshop-era; exact year unverified); WORK: Electric Elves: What Went Wrong and Why; VERSION: Six-page author manuscript; distinct from the later 2008 AI Magazine article; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Decision cost includes computation and user attention; modern evaluations add explicit monetary/resource measures.

**Development:** Retain decision-relevant total burden with explicit omissions, not false precision or token-only efficiency claims.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain decision-relevant total burden with explicit omissions, not false precision or token-only efficiency claims.; OPERATION: Report the costs material to the decision, including failed attempts, deliberation, tools, supervision and recovery; keep incomparable burdens visible rather than hiding them in a single score.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A small set of material costs suffices; do not build a comprehensive accounting system for negligible excluded costs.

**Problem:** Token price or successful-task latency alone can omit setup, failures, human repair, privacy and external effects.

**Mechanism:** Report the costs material to the decision, including failed attempts, deliberation, tools, supervision and recovery; keep incomparable burdens visible rather than hiding them in a single score.

**Trigger and context:** Economic, efficiency or autonomy-benefit claims.

### EAA-049 — Protected tests and deliberate failure exposure

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S022; DATE: 1999; WORK: Validating the DS1 Remote Agent Experiment; VERSION: ISAIRAS 1999 primary experiment report; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S034; DATE: 2024-11-24; WORK: AgentDojo: A Dynamic Environment to Evaluate Prompt Injection Attacks and Defenses for LLM Agents; VERSION: arXiv:2406.13352v3; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S038; DATE: 2026-05; WORK: Learning to Maintain Safety Through Expert Demonstrations in Settings with Unknown Constraints: A Q-Learning Perspective; VERSION: AAMAS 2026 pp.1901–1909; DOI 10.65109/RJIB1203; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S039; DATE: 2026-08-23; WORK: Physical Agentic AI: An Architecture for Orchestrating a Robot Crew with LLMs; VERSION: arXiv:2608.22657v1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Ground testing/fault injection and later sandboxed adversarial or safety-learning testbeds.

**Development:** Retain protected adversarial evaluation with transfer limits, not “passed a sandbox” as release or safety certification.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain protected adversarial evaluation with transfer limits, not “passed a sandbox” as release or safety certification.; OPERATION: Use simulation, sandboxing, controlled fixtures or appropriately bounded field tests to expose consequential faults and adverse inputs before making broad deployment claims.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A low-risk local test or static check can suffice for a harmless bounded change; avoid unrealistic simulation when direct protected evidence is cheaper.

**Problem:** Live trial-and-error may impose harms on third parties, while happy-path demonstrations miss foreseeable failure classes.

**Mechanism:** Use simulation, sandboxing, controlled fixtures or appropriately bounded field tests to expose consequential faults and adverse inputs before making broad deployment claims.

**Trigger and context:** Irreversible effects, unsafe exploration, security-sensitive tools or poorly known failure modes.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: A protected test environment, relevant fault hypotheses and a valid interpretation of what the tests do and do not establish.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Evaluator and authorising operator

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Evaluator and authorising operator; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. A protected test environment, relevant fault hypotheses and a valid interpretation of what the tests do and do not establish.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Disclose user simulation, human assistance and affected-party costs; protected tests must not externalise harm. Consumer and prerequisite: Evaluator and authorising operator. A protected test environment, relevant fault hypotheses and a valid interpretation of what the tests do and do not establish.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Failure cases are observed without silently externalising test costs, and residual transfer obligations remain visible.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** A low-risk local test or static check can suffice for a harmless bounded change; avoid unrealistic simulation when direct protected evidence is cheaper.

**Historical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Failure modes:** DS1’s many ground tests still missed a race; simulator fidelity and curated attack suites bound conclusions.; Introduced cost or harm: Test infrastructure, false confidence and incomplete rare-event coverage.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: DS1’s many ground tests still missed a race; simulator fidelity and curated attack suites bound conclusions.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C005; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S022; EAA-S034; EAA-S039; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: DS1’s many ground tests still missed a race; simulator fidelity and curated attack suites bound conclusions.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S022; EAA-S034; EAA-S039; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Test selection and environmental fidelity determine external validity; injected failures are not field-frequency estimates.

**Unresolved questions:** Which dangerous or failure-prone cases can be tested without imposing unconsented costs, and what remains untested?; Test selection and environmental fidelity determine external validity; injected failures are not field-frequency estimates.

**Property versus ceremony:** CEREMONY_ID: EAA-CE049; PROPERTY_ID: EAA-049; ARTEFACT_RITUAL_OR_BRAND: Ground testing/fault injection and later sandboxed adversarial or safety-learning testbeds.; PROTECTED_PROPERTY_AND_FAILURE: Live trial-and-error may impose harms on third parties, while happy-path demonstrations miss foreseeable failure classes.; CONSUMER: Evaluator and authorising operator; PREREQUISITES: A protected test environment, relevant fault hypotheses and a valid interpretation of what the tests do and do not establish.; MINIMAL_ADEQUATE_ALTERNATIVE: A low-risk local test or static check can suffice for a harmless bounded change; avoid unrealistic simulation when direct protected evidence is cheaper.; TRIGGER_FOR_FULLER_FORM: Irreversible effects, unsafe exploration, security-sensitive tools or poorly known failure modes.; COSTS_OF_SIMPLIFICATION: Omitting this mechanism can reintroduce the specific failure: Live trial-and-error may impose harms on third parties, while happy-path demonstrations miss foreseeable failure classes. Simplification is acceptable only while the cheaper path still establishes the relevant criterion.; OMISSION_OR_RETIREMENT_CONDITIONS: Omit or retire a dedicated implementation when this trigger no longer applies, its consumer disappears, or an existing cheaper mechanism demonstrably establishes the same criterion. Do not erase relevant genealogy or unresolved effects. Specific non-trigger: A low-risk local test or static check can suffice for a harmless bounded change; avoid unrealistic simulation when direct protected evidence is cheaper.; CONTROL_COST: Test infrastructure, false confidence and incomplete rare-event coverage.; ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY: The presence of an artefact, module, prompt or meeting is insufficient. The functional evidence is: Failure cases are observed without silently externalising test costs, and residual transfer obligations remain visible.

Earliest inspected anchor is not asserted to be the unique first origin.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S022; EAA-S034; EAA-S038; EAA-S039; THEORETICAL_SUPPORT: STRENGTH: NOT_APPLICABLE_AS_A_PROVED_THEOREM; BASIS: This record is an operational, conceptual, normative or empirical judgement; no mathematical guarantee is claimed.; SOURCE_IDS: []; COMPARATIVE_EVIDENCE: STRENGTH: MIXED_AND_SETTING_SPECIFIC; BASIS: Relevant studies or cases are recorded in the study-level evidence catalogue. They do not identify a universal causal payoff for the mature composed property.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S022; EAA-S034; EAA-S038; EAA-S039; FIELD_OR_DEPLOYMENT_EVIDENCE: STRENGTH: LIMITED_AUTHOR_REPORTED; BASIS: Field/robot programme observations support feasibility or concrete failure modes under their actual settings. Target evidence must establish the relevant observability, authority, timing, effect and evaluation conditions.; SYNTHESIS_STATUS: STRENGTH: RESEARCHER_INTERPRETATION; BASIS: Retain protected adversarial evaluation with transfer limits, not “passed a sandbox” as release or safety certification.

### EAA-050 — Monitor material assurance assumptions at runtime

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S042; DATE: 2018; WORK: Recognising Assumption Violations in Autonomous Systems Verification; VERSION: AAMAS 2018 extended abstract pp.1933–1935; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S022; DATE: 1999; WORK: Validating the DS1 Remote Agent Experiment; VERSION: ISAIRAS 1999 primary experiment report; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Runtime recognition of environmental assumption violations used in static verification.

**Development:** Retain useful assumption monitoring with explicit response and coverage, not an all-purpose wrapper that proves the entire world safe.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain useful assumption monitoring with explicit response and coverage, not an all-purpose wrapper that proves the entire world safe.; OPERATION: Monitor observable assumptions that matter to the guarantee and connect detected violations to an appropriate re-evaluation, fallback or limitation of the claim.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: No runtime monitor is required for an immutable established condition, or where an existing boundary check already detects the relevant violation.

**Problem:** A verified decision component can operate outside the environmental conditions under which it was verified.

**Mechanism:** Monitor observable assumptions that matter to the guarantee and connect detected violations to an appropriate re-evaluation, fallback or limitation of the claim.

**Trigger and context:** Assurance depends on environmental behaviour that can change or fail.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: Explicit assumptions, observable indicators and a consumer capable of acting on a violation.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Runtime monitor, executor and assurance reviewer

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Runtime monitor, executor and assurance reviewer; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Explicit assumptions, observable indicators and a consumer capable of acting on a violation.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Disclose user simulation, human assistance and affected-party costs; protected tests must not externalise harm. Consumer and prerequisite: Runtime monitor, executor and assurance reviewer. Explicit assumptions, observable indicators and a consumer capable of acting on a violation.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** A violated monitored assumption invalidates the relevant assurance claim or changes operation rather than remaining a silent discrepancy.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** No runtime monitor is required for an immutable established condition, or where an existing boundary check already detects the relevant violation.

**Historical disposition:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

**Failure modes:** The monitor inherits its own model and observation limits; detecting a violation does not itself establish a safe recovery.; Introduced cost or harm: Runtime overhead, nuisance alarms and monitor/environment mismatch.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: The monitor inherits its own model and observation limits; detecting a violation does not itself establish a safe recovery.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C005; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S042; EAA-S022; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: The monitor inherits its own model and observation limits; detecting a violation does not itself establish a safe recovery.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S042; EAA-S022; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Unobservable assumptions and unmodelled failures remain outside monitor coverage.

**Unresolved questions:** Which assumption could become false during use, how would that be noticed, and what changes then?; Unobservable assumptions and unmodelled failures remain outside monitor coverage.

Simplification is acceptable only while the cheaper path still establishes the relevant criterion.; OMISSION_OR_RETIREMENT_CONDITIONS: Omit or retire a dedicated implementation when this trigger no longer applies, its consumer disappears, or an existing cheaper mechanism demonstrably establishes the same criterion. ### EAA-051 — Bound formal claims to model and implementation obligations

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S009; DATE: 1995; WORK: BDI Agents: From Theory to Practice; VERSION: ICMAS 1995 pp.312–319; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S012; DATE: 1998; WORK: Planning and Acting in Partially Observable Stochastic Domains; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 101, 99–134; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S024; DATE: 2016; WORK: Safely Interruptible Agents; VERSION: UAI 2016 pp.557–566; author PDF; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S027; DATE: 1992; WORK: Q-learning; VERSION: Machine Learning 8, 279–292; DOI 10.1007/BF00992698; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S042; DATE: 2018; WORK: Recognising Assumption Violations in Autonomous Systems Verification; VERSION: AAMAS 2018 extended abstract pp.1933–1935; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S053; DATE: 2016; WORK: Self-Modification of Policy and Utility Function in Rational Agents; VERSION: AGI 2016 conference version, 10 pages; DOI 10.1007/978-3-319-41649-6_1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Model-relative logics, convergence theorems and interpreter approximations with separate practical obligations.

**Development:** Retain formal evidence as one partition of assurance rather than an average confidence score or a substitute for environment validation.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain formal evidence as one partition of assurance rather than an average confidence score or a substitute for environment validation.; OPERATION: State the model, property and assumptions; separately establish the implementation relation and relevant environmental adequacy, including approximation and concurrency gaps.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: For a modest empirical claim, report the evidence without pretending a formal theorem is needed; direct testing can be adequate within its scope.

**Problem:** A mathematical guarantee can be asserted for an implemented agent whose state, timing or environment violates the formal assumptions.

**Mechanism:** State the model, property and assumptions; separately establish the implementation relation and relevant environmental adequacy, including approximation and concurrency gaps.

**Trigger and context:** Invoking a proof, verified component, convergence theorem or formally specified agent language.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: An identifiable formal result and a claim about the real implementation narrow enough to examine.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Verifier, implementer and assurance consumer

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Verifier, implementer and assurance consumer; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. An identifiable formal result and a claim about the real implementation narrow enough to examine.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Disclose user simulation, human assistance and affected-party costs; protected tests must not externalise harm. Consumer and prerequisite: Verifier, implementer and assurance consumer. An identifiable formal result and a claim about the real implementation narrow enough to examine.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** The claimed guarantee is neither inflated beyond its assumptions nor rejected merely because a non-conforming implementation failed.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** For a modest empirical claim, report the evidence without pretending a formal theorem is needed; direct testing can be adequate within its scope.

**Historical disposition:** STRONGLY_RETAINED

**Failure modes:** Formal assurance can be strong locally but weak in deployment; proving the abstraction relation can dominate the engineering effort.; Introduced cost or harm: Modelling, proof and refinement-validation effort.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Formal assurance can be strong locally but weak in deployment; proving the abstraction relation can dominate the engineering effort.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C005; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S009; EAA-S022; EAA-S041; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Formal assurance can be strong locally but weak in deployment; proving the abstraction relation can dominate the engineering effort.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S009; EAA-S022; EAA-S041; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Proof validity, abstraction correctness and external validity are separate questions; this study does not independently re-prove every cited theorem.

**Unresolved questions:** What exactly is proved, and what evidence connects its assumptions and transitions to this running system?; Proof validity, abstraction correctness and external validity are separate questions; this study does not independently re-prove every cited theorem.

<a id="eaa-052"></a>
### EAA-052 — Untrusted observations do not confer instruction authority

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S034; DATE: 2024-11-24; WORK: AgentDojo: A Dynamic Environment to Evaluate Prompt Injection Attacks and Defenses for LLM Agents; VERSION: arXiv:2406.13352v3; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Adversarial instructions embedded in externally returned tool content.

**Development:** Retain input-integrity and authority separation as a contemporary translation, not a claim that older belief annotations solve prompt injection.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain input-integrity and authority separation as a contemporary translation, not a claim that older belief annotations solve prompt injection.; OPERATION: Treat external content according to its evidential and authority role; constrain consequential tool use independently of persuasive wording and evaluate both utility and attack resistance.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: No language-level injection mechanism is needed for a closed typed sensor value with no instruction interpretation, though ordinary input validation may still matter.

**Problem:** Tool results or retrieved text can contain adversarial instructions that redirect the agent beyond the delegated task.

**Mechanism:** Treat external content according to its evidential and authority role; constrain consequential tool use independently of persuasive wording and evaluate both utility and attack resistance.

**Trigger and context:** Language-model agents consume web pages, documents, messages or tool results from untrusted sources.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: Identified trust boundaries, controlled capabilities and tests for relevant adversarial observations.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Tool boundary, action selector and security evaluator

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Tool boundary, action selector and security evaluator; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Identified trust boundaries, controlled capabilities and tests for relevant adversarial observations.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Helpful initiative remains delegated; untrusted content cannot legitimise new authority, and human handoff needs actual readiness. Consumer and prerequisite: Tool boundary, action selector and security evaluator. Identified trust boundaries, controlled capabilities and tests for relevant adversarial observations.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Untrusted text cannot legitimately expand task authority merely by claiming to be a higher-priority instruction.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** No language-level injection mechanism is needed for a closed typed sensor value with no instruction interpretation, though ordinary input validation may still matter.

### EAA-053 — Language-model reasoning–action feedback

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S030; DATE: 2023; WORK: ReAct: Synergizing Reasoning and Acting in Language Models; VERSION: arXiv:2210.03629v3; initial submission 2022; ICLR 2023 work; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** ReAct interleaves generated reasoning and actual tool/environment observations.

**Development:** Retain feedback-coupled language action as one implementation family, not as a new origin of agency or evidence of BDI semantics.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain feedback-coupled language action as one implementation family, not as a new origin of agency or evidence of BDI semantics.; OPERATION: Interleave task-relevant generated reasoning or action selection with real tool/environment observations and use them to revise subsequent choices, with capability and authority boundaries outside mere narration.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A fixed workflow, direct tool invocation or reactive policy is sufficient when open-ended selection offers no demonstrated benefit.

**Problem:** A language-only chain can invent facts or continue a plan without observing what its tools actually did.

**Mechanism:** Interleave task-relevant generated reasoning or action selection with real tool/environment observations and use them to revise subsequent choices, with capability and authority boundaries outside mere narration.

**Trigger and context:** Language models choose multi-step tool actions under changing information.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: Grounded tool interfaces, interpretable feedback, bounded action selection and an adequate completion oracle.; PROPERTY_IDS: EAA-021; EAA-023; EAA-026; EAA-045; EAA-052; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Language-model action selector and executor

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Language-model action selector and executor; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Grounded tool interfaces, interpretable feedback, bounded action selection and an adequate completion oracle.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Helpful initiative remains delegated; untrusted content cannot legitimise new authority, and human handoff needs actual readiness. Consumer and prerequisite: Language-model action selector and executor. Grounded tool interfaces, interpretable feedback, bounded action selection and an adequate completion oracle.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** The next action is responsive to actual observations rather than only the model’s predicted narrative.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** A fixed workflow, direct tool invocation or reactive policy is sufficient when open-ended selection offers no demonstrated benefit.

**Historical disposition:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

**Failure modes:** ReAct’s selected benchmarks do not establish universal superiority; interleaving creates more opportunities to consume malicious tool text or costly retries.; Introduced cost or harm: Tokens, latency, attack surface and increased branching.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: ReAct’s selected benchmarks do not establish universal superiority; interleaving creates more opportunities to consume malicious tool text or costly retries.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C012; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S034; EAA-S035; EAA-S036; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: ReAct’s selected benchmarks do not establish universal superiority; interleaving creates more opportunities to consume malicious tool text or costly retries.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S034; EAA-S035; EAA-S036; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Model capability, prompt, environment and action schema determine results; a reasoning trace can be inaccurate.

**Unresolved questions:** What real observation changes the next action, and does this freedom outperform a constrained alternative?; Model capability, prompt, environment and action schema determine results; a reasoning trace can be inaccurate.

### EAA-054 — External-feedback-anchored reflection

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S031; DATE: 2023-10-10; WORK: Reflexion: Language Agents with Verbal Reinforcement Learning; VERSION: arXiv:2303.11366v4; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Reflexion adds an evaluator and limited verbal memory between task attempts.

**Development:** Retain conditional feedback-driven revision, not permanent introspection or guaranteed self-improvement.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain conditional feedback-driven revision, not permanent introspection or guaranteed self-improvement.; OPERATION: Use a bounded review of observed outcomes to formulate a testable change for the next attempt; keep feedback validity separate from the generated lesson and assess actual improvement.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A direct correction or fixed retry can suffice; omit reflection when there is no new evidence or no expected benefit.

**Problem:** An agent may repeat a mistake, but generating a reflective story can also consolidate a false diagnosis.

**Mechanism:** Use a bounded review of observed outcomes to formulate a testable change for the next attempt; keep feedback validity separate from the generated lesson and assess actual improvement.

**Trigger and context:** A recoverable repeated task with useful feedback and enough budget for another attempt.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: An adequate evaluator, comparable subsequent attempt and memory/change scope that does not override task authority.; PROPERTY_IDS: EAA-023; EAA-026; EAA-030; EAA-045; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Revision process and evaluator

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Revision process and evaluator; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. An adequate evaluator, comparable subsequent attempt and memory/change scope that does not override task authority.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Helpful initiative remains delegated; untrusted content cannot legitimise new authority, and human handoff needs actual readiness. Consumer and prerequisite: Revision process and evaluator. An adequate evaluator, comparable subsequent attempt and memory/change scope that does not override task authority.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** The revised attempt is judged by external task evidence rather than the persuasiveness of its reflection.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** A direct correction or fixed retry can suffice; omit reflection when there is no new evidence or no expected benefit.

**Historical disposition:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

**Failure modes:** Reflexion reports false self-test positives, a negative MBPP comparison and stalled WebShop improvement; repeated resets and trials confound single-attempt claims.; Introduced cost or harm: Extra attempts, token cost and contaminated memory.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Reflexion reports false self-test positives, a negative MBPP comparison and stalled WebShop improvement; repeated resets and trials confound single-attempt claims.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C009; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S031; EAA-S035; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Reflexion reports false self-test positives, a negative MBPP comparison and stalled WebShop improvement; repeated resets and trials confound single-attempt claims.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S031; EAA-S035; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Quality of evaluation, task transfer and available retries are material; natural-language memories can preserve wrong lessons.

**Unresolved questions:** What new external evidence supports the lesson, and does the next attempt actually improve under a fair comparison?; Quality of evaluation, task transfer and available retries are material; natural-language memories can preserve wrong lessons.

### EAA-055 — Re-evaluate consequential model and tool version changes

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S035; DATE: 2024-07-01; WORK: AI Agents That Matter; VERSION: arXiv:2407.01502v1; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S036; DATE: 2024-10-29; WORK: Agentless: Demystifying LLM-based Software Engineering Agents; VERSION: arXiv:2407.01489v2; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S048; DATE: 2026-07-10; WORK: Measuring AI Ability to Complete Long Software Tasks; VERSION: arXiv:2503.14499v4; initial submission March 2025; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S042; DATE: 2018; WORK: Recognising Assumption Violations in Autonomous Systems Verification; VERSION: AAMAS 2018 extended abstract pp.1933–1935; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Benchmark results tied to particular model/tool configurations; analytical extension to impact-sensitive reassessment.

**Development:** Retain consequence-sensitive reassessment rather than permanent retesting of everything or trust in a marketing version label.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain consequence-sensitive reassessment rather than permanent retesting of everything or trust in a marketing version label.; OPERATION: Identify changes that can affect action, interpretation or evaluation semantics and reassess the relevant prior claims on targeted regression and transfer cases.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A syntax-only or demonstrably irrelevant change need not trigger a full campaign; reuse valid evidence with a justified impact boundary.

**Problem:** An unchanged prompt or application can behave differently after its underlying model, tool contract or memory policy changes.

**Mechanism:** Identify changes that can affect action, interpretation or evaluation semantics and reassess the relevant prior claims on targeted regression and transfer cases.

**Trigger and context:** Changed models, prompts, tools, environment APIs, retrieval rules or learned policies used in consequential decisions.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: Known versions or change indicators, traceable prior claims and appropriate regression cases.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

Known versions or change indicators, traceable prior claims and appropriate regression cases.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Reused assurance is tied to the actual configuration; unsupported continuity is not inferred from a stable product name.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** A syntax-only or demonstrably irrelevant change need not trigger a full campaign; reuse valid evidence with a justified impact boundary.

**Historical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Failure modes:** Closed systems may hide changes; reproducibility is limited by unavailable historical models and shared benchmark contamination.; Introduced cost or harm: Regression cost, delayed use and incomplete access to changed internals.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Closed systems may hide changes; reproducibility is limited by unavailable historical models and shared benchmark contamination.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C012; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S035; EAA-S036; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Impact analysis can miss emergent interactions; a new version is neither automatically safer nor automatically worse.

**Unresolved questions:** Which previous claim can this change invalidate, and what evidence justifies retaining it?; Impact analysis can miss emergent interactions; a new version is neither automatically safer nor automatically worse.

<a id="eaa-056"></a>
### EAA-056 — Human-likeness as a general competence or consciousness proxy

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S001; DATE: 1995-06; WORK: Intelligent Agents: Theory and Practice; VERSION: Knowledge Engineering Review 10(2), 115–152; author HTML; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S010; DATE: 1993; WORK: Agent-oriented programming; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 60, 51–92; full journal scan; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S050; DATE: 2023-08-06; WORK: Generative Agents: Interactive Simulacra of Human Behavior; VERSION: arXiv:2304.03442v2; UIST 2023 DOI 10.1145/3586183.3606763; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Analytical candidate inference from anthropomorphic appearance to general competence or consciousness; not an endorsed theorem.

**Development:** Retain separate predicates and task-specific tests; reject the general proxy without dismissing useful human-facing simulation.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain separate predicates and task-specific tests; reject the general proxy without dismissing useful human-facing simulation.; OPERATION: The candidate inference would infer broad capability or subjectivity from believable persona or mental-state vocabulary; that inference is not retained.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Evaluate actual task action and treat believability as a legitimate separate outcome only where the task calls for it.

**Problem:** Human-like language or behaviour can be mistaken for dependable task competence, consciousness or moral status.

**Mechanism:** The candidate inference would infer broad capability or subjectivity from believable persona or mental-state vocabulary; that inference is not retained.

**Trigger and context:** Anthropomorphic interpretation of an agent demonstration.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: No sufficient bridge from believability to the stronger claim is established by the inspected agent studies.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Public interpreter and evaluator

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Public interpreter and evaluator; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. No sufficient bridge from believability to the stronger claim is established by the inspected agent studies.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Social ability is required by some definitions but does not mean every task needs negotiation; user autonomy remains distinct. Consumer and prerequisite: Public interpreter and evaluator. No sufficient bridge from believability to the stronger claim is established by the inspected agent studies.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Believability results remain labelled believability; no unsupported personhood or competence inference is made.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Evaluate actual task action and treat believability as a legitimate separate outcome only where the task calls for it.

**Historical disposition:** NO_GENERAL_PROPERTY

**Failure modes:** Generative Agents measures believability in a simulation, not consciousness or dependable operation. Rejecting this inference does not prove absence of consciousness.; Introduced cost or harm: Misplaced trust, manipulation or unwarranted metaphysical conclusions.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Generative Agents measures believability in a simulation, not consciousness or dependable operation. Rejecting this inference does not prove absence of consciousness.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C001; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S043; EAA-S050; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Generative Agents measures believability in a simulation, not consciousness or dependable operation. Rejecting this inference does not prove absence of consciousness.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S043; EAA-S050; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: true; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** A philosophical consciousness criterion is outside the evidence and cannot be smuggled in through engineering vocabulary.

**Unresolved questions:** What evidence supports the claimed capability beyond the appearance of human-like behaviour?; A philosophical consciousness criterion is outside the evidence and cannot be smuggled in through engineering vocabulary.

### EAA-057 — Maximum autonomy and persistence are always preferable

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S017; DATE: 2008-10; WORK: Autonomy Levels for Unmanned Systems (ALFUS) Framework, Volume I: Terminology; VERSION: NIST Special Publication 1011-I-2.0, Version 2.0; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S019; DATE: 1999; WORK: Principles of Mixed-Initiative User Interfaces; VERSION: CHI 1999; author manuscript; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Analytical universalisation that more independence and longer pursuit are always better.

**Development:** Replace monotonic autonomy with conditional capability, authority and architecture selection.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Replace monotonic autonomy with conditional capability, authority and architecture selection.; OPERATION: The rejected rule would monotonically increase initiative, duration and freedom regardless of task evidence.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use the minimum adequate delegated freedom and preserve task-relative intervention and retirement.

**Problem:** Increasing independence may remove useful correction, prolong obsolete tasks or amplify harmful actions.

**Mechanism:** The rejected rule would monotonically increase initiative, duration and freedom regardless of task evidence.

**Trigger and context:** Claims that less oversight or greater persistence is inherently a maturity gain.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: No general monotonic benefit assumption is supported; appropriate scope and human readiness must be established instead.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Delegator and architecture decision maker

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Delegator and architecture decision maker; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. No general monotonic benefit assumption is supported; appropriate scope and human readiness must be established instead.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Social ability is required by some definitions but does not mean every task needs negotiation; user autonomy remains distinct. Consumer and prerequisite: Delegator and architecture decision maker. No general monotonic benefit assumption is supported; appropriate scope and human readiness must be established instead.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Autonomy choices are justified by the task rather than a universal score or prestige claim.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Use the minimum adequate delegated freedom and preserve task-relative intervention and retirement.

**Historical disposition:** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

**Failure modes:** Electric Elves non-response failures, human-autonomy concerns and cheaper fixed workflows directly undermine universal benefit claims.; Introduced cost or harm: Overreach, burdens on others and prolonged ineffective action.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Electric Elves non-response failures, human-autonomy concerns and cheaper fixed workflows directly undermine universal benefit claims.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C003; EAA-C007; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S015; EAA-S020; EAA-S043; EAA-S036; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Electric Elves non-response failures, human-autonomy concerns and cheaper fixed workflows directly undermine universal benefit claims.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S015; EAA-S020; EAA-S043; EAA-S036; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: true; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** This does not imply manual control always wins; oversight can be costly or infeasible.

**Unresolved questions:** What specific gain justifies this added autonomy, and which newly introduced failure must be controlled?; This does not imply manual control always wins; oversight can be costly or infeasible.

### EAA-058 — A complete central world model is universally necessary

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S005; DATE: 1991; WORK: Intelligence without representation; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 47, 139–159; author reformatted copy; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S012; DATE: 1998; WORK: Planning and Acting in Partially Observable Stochastic Domains; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 101, 99–134; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Analytical universalisation of comprehensive central modelling as necessary for every agent.

**Development:** Replace universal completeness with task-relative representation adequacy and explicit alternatives.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Replace universal completeness with task-relative representation adequacy and explicit alternatives.; OPERATION: The rejected rule requires comprehensive central reconstruction before adequate agency is possible.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use direct feedback or a small sufficient state estimate when it supports the required discrimination.

**Problem:** Compulsory complete representation adds cost and delays without necessarily improving action.

**Mechanism:** The rejected rule requires comprehensive central reconstruction before adequate agency is possible.

**Trigger and context:** Architecture claims treating modelling richness as a prerequisite for every task.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: No completeness prerequisite is retained; richer modelling requires a task-specific need and feasible maintenance.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Architect

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Architect; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. No completeness prerequisite is retained; richer modelling requires a task-specific need and feasible maintenance.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Override, handback and authority changes must have real operational effects across active layers. Consumer and prerequisite: Architect. No completeness prerequisite is retained; richer modelling requires a task-specific need and feasible maintenance.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Adequate reactive agents are not excluded by definition merely because they lack a comprehensive symbolic world.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Use direct feedback or a small sufficient state estimate when it supports the required discrimination.

**Historical disposition:** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

**Failure modes:** Brooks’ examples challenge necessity for some tasks, not every model; POMDP settings still need enough state to resolve relevant uncertainty.; Introduced cost or harm: Unnecessary modelling, staleness and computation.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Brooks’ examples challenge necessity for some tasks, not every model; POMDP settings still need enough state to resolve relevant uncertainty.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C002; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S005; EAA-S014; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Brooks’ examples challenge necessity for some tasks, not every model; POMDP settings still need enough state to resolve relevant uncertainty.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S005; EAA-S014; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: true; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Adequacy must be demonstrated; rejecting completeness does not justify ignoring hidden state.

**Unresolved questions:** Which action-relevant distinction truly requires the proposed extra model?; Adequacy must be demonstrated; rejecting completeness does not justify ignoring hidden state.

### EAA-059 — Representation-free reaction is universally sufficient

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S005; DATE: 1991; WORK: Intelligence without representation; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 47, 139–159; author reformatted copy; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S012; DATE: 1998; WORK: Planning and Acting in Partially Observable Stochastic Domains; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 101, 99–134; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S046; DATE: 1995; WORK: Experiences with an Architecture for Intelligent, Reactive Agents; VERSION: ATAL 1995 workshop author copy; 17 pages; distinct later journal version; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Analytical universalisation of representation-free reactive sufficiency.

**Development:** Retain direct control as an alternative while rejecting its universalisation.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain direct control as an alternative while rejecting its universalisation.; OPERATION: The rejected rule would dispense with task-relevant internal state or anticipation regardless of observability and horizon.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use reaction where sufficient; add only the state, memory or deliberation that resolves a demonstrated inadequacy.

**Problem:** A purely reactive policy may alias materially different hidden states or miss long-horizon dependencies.

**Mechanism:** The rejected rule would dispense with task-relevant internal state or anticipation regardless of observability and horizon.

**Trigger and context:** Claims that environmental feedback alone always removes the need for memory or planning.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: No universal sufficiency assumption is retained; current observations must be adequate for the chosen policy.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Architect and evaluator

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Architect and evaluator; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. No universal sufficiency assumption is retained; current observations must be adequate for the chosen policy.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Override, handback and authority changes must have real operational effects across active layers. Consumer and prerequisite: Architect and evaluator. No universal sufficiency assumption is retained; current observations must be adequate for the chosen policy.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Reactive success is scoped to its demonstrated conditions rather than extrapolated to all tasks.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Use reaction where sufficient; add only the state, memory or deliberation that resolves a demonstrated inadequacy.

**Historical disposition:** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

**Failure modes:** Partial-observation decision models exhibit why history matters; hybrid architectures address problems that direct reaction alone does not settle.; Introduced cost or harm: Missed dependencies, oscillation and failure under perceptual aliasing.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Partial-observation decision models exhibit why history matters; hybrid architectures address problems that direct reaction alone does not settle.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C002; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S012; EAA-S046; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Partial-observation decision models exhibit why history matters; hybrid architectures address problems that direct reaction alone does not settle.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S012; EAA-S046; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: true; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** The argument concerns action adequacy, not metaphysical claims about representation.

**Unresolved questions:** Can identical current observations require different actions because of history or hidden state?; The argument concerns action adequacy, not metaphysical claims about representation.
