### EAA-060 — Proxy maximisation is sufficient objective fulfilment

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S025; DATE: 2022; WORK: Goal Misgeneralization in Deep Reinforcement Learning; VERSION: ICML 2022, PMLR 162, 12004–12019; final proceedings version; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S033; DATE: 2024-06; WORK: τ-bench: A Benchmark for Tool-Agent-User Interaction in Real-World Domains; VERSION: arXiv:2406.12045v1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Analytical universalisation of measured reward as complete intended fulfilment.

**Development:** Replace unconditional proxy sufficiency with EAA-034’s target distinction and EAA-045’s oracle adequacy.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Replace unconditional proxy sufficiency with EAA-034’s target distinction and EAA-045’s oracle adequacy.; OPERATION: The rejected inference equates maximum measured reward with complete intended success in the target environment.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use an adequate direct predicate where it genuinely covers the claim; otherwise preserve the proxy’s known limits.

**Problem:** Optimisation can succeed exactly at a metric while missing the desired task or violating protected means.

**Mechanism:** The rejected inference equates maximum measured reward with complete intended success in the target environment.

**Trigger and context:** Claims of reliable agency based solely on a training reward or benchmark score.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: No general equivalence is established; task/proxy correspondence needs separate evidence.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Learning designer and evaluator

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Learning designer and evaluator; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. No general equivalence is established; task/proxy correspondence needs separate evidence.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Human correction is informative but fallible; persistent memory also raises privacy and consent questions. Consumer and prerequisite: Learning designer and evaluator. No general equivalence is established; task/proxy correspondence needs separate evidence.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** A score is not promoted to full fulfilment when counterexamples remain.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Use an adequate direct predicate where it genuinely covers the claim; otherwise preserve the proxy’s known limits.

**Historical disposition:** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

**Failure modes:** Controlled RL and τ-bench provide concrete ways proxy success and intended compliance diverge.; Introduced cost or harm: Reward gaming, objective drift and false assurance.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Controlled RL and τ-bench provide concrete ways proxy success and intended compliance diverge.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C010; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S025; EAA-S033; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Controlled RL and τ-bench provide concrete ways proxy success and intended compliance diverge.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S025; EAA-S033; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: true; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Some bounded scores may be adequate; the rejected claim is universal, not a rejection of all measurement.

**Unresolved questions:** Which unmeasured requirement could be sacrificed while improving the reported score?; Some bounded scores may be adequate; the rejected claim is universal, not a rejection of all measurement.

<a id="eaa-061"></a>
### EAA-061 — Mandatory continuous reflection

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S031; DATE: 2023-10-10; WORK: Reflexion: Language Agents with Verbal Reinforcement Learning; VERSION: arXiv:2303.11366v4; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Analytical extrapolation from reflective methods to a mandatory always-on ritual.

**Development:** Superseded by EAA-054 external-feedback-anchored reflection and EAA-028 value-sensitive deliberation.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Superseded by EAA-054 external-feedback-anchored reflection and EAA-028 value-sensitive deliberation.; OPERATION: The superseded form reflects after every event or indefinitely, independent of feedback quality and marginal value.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use a direct correction, fixed policy or bounded externally evaluated reflection only when it is expected to help.

**Problem:** An obligatory reflection loop consumes resources and can amplify incorrect feedback without improving decisions.

**Mechanism:** The superseded form reflects after every event or indefinitely, independent of feedback quality and marginal value.

**Trigger and context:** Adding always-on self-critique or repeated self-improvement prompts.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: Useful feedback, a feasible next attempt and a task-specific budget are prerequisites for the retained replacement.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Revision designer and deliberation scheduler

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Revision designer and deliberation scheduler; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Useful feedback, a feasible next attempt and a task-specific budget are prerequisites for the retained replacement.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Helpful initiative remains delegated; untrusted content cannot legitimise new authority, and human handoff needs actual readiness. Consumer and prerequisite: Revision designer and deliberation scheduler. Useful feedback, a feasible next attempt and a task-specific budget are prerequisites for the retained replacement.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Reflection is invoked conditionally and stopped when it fails to produce useful evidence or improvement.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Use a direct correction, fixed policy or bounded externally evaluated reflection only when it is expected to help.

**Historical disposition:** SUPERSEDED_BY_STRONGER_FORM

**Failure modes:** Reflexion’s negative MBPP result and stalled WebShop trials contradict an unconditional improvement assumption; metareasoning shows opportunity costs.; Introduced cost or harm: Tokens, latency, local minima and contaminated memory.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Reflexion’s negative MBPP result and stalled WebShop trials contradict an unconditional improvement assumption; metareasoning shows opportunity costs.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C009; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S031; EAA-S035; EAA-S014; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Reflexion’s negative MBPP result and stalled WebShop trials contradict an unconditional improvement assumption; metareasoning shows opportunity costs.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S031; EAA-S035; EAA-S014; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** A larger budget may help some tasks but does not prove every additional reflective step is worthwhile.

**Unresolved questions:** What would be lost by omitting this reflection, and what evidence justifies its next iteration?; A larger budget may help some tasks but does not prove every additional reflective step is worthwhile.

<a id="eaa-062"></a>
### EAA-062 — General assurance for open-ended self-revision

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S053; DATE: 2016; WORK: Self-Modification of Policy and Utility Function in Rational Agents; VERSION: AGI 2016 conference version, 10 pages; DOI 10.1007/978-3-319-41649-6_1; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S026; DATE: 2017-01-25; WORK: Overcoming catastrophic forgetting in neural networks; VERSION: arXiv:1612.00796v2; initial submission December 2016; distinct PNAS edition; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Open research claim of broad assurance for agents that can change their own policies and evaluative criteria.

**Development:** Retain EAA-070 as a narrow formal resource while leaving general open-ended assurance unresolved.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain EAA-070 as a narrow formal resource while leaving general open-ended assurance unresolved.; OPERATION: Investigate whether and under what implementable conditions broad self-revision can preserve justified constraints, learning responsiveness and corrigibility across unknown environments; no general mechanism is certified by this corpus.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Bound the change surface, retain an external or predecessor-authorised criterion and use evaluated fixed mechanisms rather than claiming broad self-assurance.

**Problem:** An agent that changes its own policy or evaluation mechanism may invalidate the evidence used to trust later changes.

**Mechanism:** Investigate whether and under what implementable conditions broad self-revision can preserve justified constraints, learning responsiveness and corrigibility across unknown environments; no general mechanism is certified by this corpus.

**Trigger and context:** Claims that an open-ended self-modifying agent can generally warrant its own continued acceptability.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: An acceptable revision criterion, trustworthy evaluation and implementation/environment assumptions; their general sufficiency remains unresolved.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Researcher and authorised revision reviewer

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Researcher and authorised revision reviewer; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. An acceptable revision criterion, trustworthy evaluation and implementation/environment assumptions; their general sufficiency remains unresolved.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Human correction is informative but fallible; persistent memory also raises privacy and consent questions. Consumer and prerequisite: Researcher and authorised revision reviewer. An acceptable revision criterion, trustworthy evaluation and implementation/environment assumptions; their general sufficiency remains unresolved.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** The current result is an explicit evidence boundary, not a promised guarantee or a claim of impossibility.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Bound the change surface, retain an external or predecessor-authorised criterion and use evaluated fixed mechanisms rather than claiming broad self-assurance.

**Historical disposition:** UNRESOLVED

**Failure modes:** The formal result can preserve a wrong initial utility and assumes optimal anticipation; finite evaluations cannot by themselves certify every future self-change.; Introduced cost or harm: Evaluation regress, rigidity, hidden goal drift and misplaced confidence.

### EAA-063 — Belief/intention prompts without operational semantics

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S010; DATE: 1993; WORK: Agent-oriented programming; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 60, 51–92; full journal scan; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S009; DATE: 1995; WORK: BDI Agents: From Theory to Practice; VERSION: ICMAS 1995 pp.312–319; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S030; DATE: 2023; WORK: ReAct: Synergizing Reasoning and Acting in Language Models; VERSION: arXiv:2210.03629v3; initial submission 2022; ICLR 2023 work; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Mental-state labels or prompting presented without an operative transition/consumer relation.

**Development:** Reject the ceremony while retaining EAA-003’s computational grounding and conditional EAA-018.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Reject the ceremony while retaining EAA-003’s computational grounding and conditional EAA-018.; OPERATION: The ceremonial form names mental states without defined storage, updates, commitment rules, action consumers or conformance evidence.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use direct task/state descriptions or implement and test the actual semantics when BDI behaviour is needed.

**Problem:** An instruction to “maintain beliefs and intentions” can substitute vocabulary for a working mechanism.

**Mechanism:** The ceremonial form names mental states without defined storage, updates, commitment rules, action consumers or conformance evidence.

**Trigger and context:** Claims that a prompt template alone implements BDI or persistent intention.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: No property is established by the label; actual transitions and their consumer are prerequisites.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Implementer and reviewer

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Implementer and reviewer; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. No property is established by the label; actual transitions and their consumer are prerequisites.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Helpful initiative remains delegated; untrusted content cannot legitimise new authority, and human handoff needs actual readiness. Consumer and prerequisite: Implementer and reviewer. No property is established by the label; actual transitions and their consumer are prerequisites.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Conceptual labels are explicitly separated from implemented capabilities.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Use direct task/state descriptions or implement and test the actual semantics when BDI behaviour is needed.

**Historical disposition:** CEREMONY_NOT_GENERAL_PROPERTY

**Failure modes:** Prompting can shape useful behaviour empirically, but that is not proof of the corresponding formal architecture.; Introduced cost or harm: Misleading documentation, false confidence and untested persistence.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Prompting can shape useful behaviour empirically, but that is not proof of the corresponding formal architecture.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C001; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S010; EAA-S009; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Assumption sensitivity:** This does not imply prompts cannot implement part of an agent; evidence must support the specific claimed behaviour.

**Unresolved questions:** Where are the claimed belief and intention transitions actually enforced or demonstrated?; This does not imply prompts cannot implement part of an agent; evidence must support the specific claimed behaviour.

### EAA-064 — Leaderboard success as deployment guarantee

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S032; DATE: 2024; WORK: WebArena: A Realistic Web Environment for Building Autonomous Agents; VERSION: arXiv:2307.13854v4; initial submission 2023; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S033; DATE: 2024-06; WORK: τ-bench: A Benchmark for Tool-Agent-User Interaction in Real-World Domains; VERSION: arXiv:2406.12045v1; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S035; DATE: 2024-07-01; WORK: AI Agents That Matter; VERSION: arXiv:2407.01502v1; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S048; DATE: 2026-07-10; WORK: Measuring AI Ability to Complete Long Software Tasks; VERSION: arXiv:2503.14499v4; initial submission March 2025; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Benchmark score or fitted task horizon promoted to an unrestricted deployment guarantee.

**Development:** Retain benchmarks as scoped evidence while rejecting universal deployment certification.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain benchmarks as scoped evidence while rejecting universal deployment certification.; OPERATION: The rejected inference promotes a score, best trajectory or fitted time horizon to a general service or deployment guarantee.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use the score as bounded evidence and test the material differences of the intended application.

**Problem:** A favourable benchmark rank can be interpreted as dependable operation on untested real tasks.

**Mechanism:** The rejected inference promotes a score, best trajectory or fitted time horizon to a general service or deployment guarantee.

**Trigger and context:** Marketing, selecting or trusting an agent using benchmark results alone.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: No general transfer guarantee follows; target conditions and success requirements must be established separately.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Benchmark consumer and deployment decision maker

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Benchmark consumer and deployment decision maker; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. No general transfer guarantee follows; target conditions and success requirements must be established separately.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Disclose user simulation, human assistance and affected-party costs; protected tests must not externalise harm. Consumer and prerequisite: Benchmark consumer and deployment decision maker. No general transfer guarantee follows; target conditions and success requirements must be established separately.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Benchmark scope, repetitions, costs and oracle omissions remain visible in the claim.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Use the score as bounded evidence and test the material differences of the intended application.

**Historical disposition:** NO_GENERAL_PROPERTY

**Assumption sensitivity:** Distribution shift, hidden human support, leakage and rare harms can dominate target performance.

**Unresolved questions:** Which differences between this benchmark and the intended task invalidate the claimed guarantee?; Distribution shift, hidden human support, leakage and rare harms can dominate target performance.

### EAA-066 — Software–physical translation needs new obligations

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S045; DATE: 1996; WORK: Planning-Based Control of Software Agents; VERSION: AIPS 1996 proceedings, pp.268–274; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S022; DATE: 1999; WORK: Validating the DS1 Remote Agent Experiment; VERSION: ISAIRAS 1999 primary experiment report; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S049; DATE: 2022-08-16; WORK: Do As I Can, Not As I Say: Grounding Language in Robotic Affordances; VERSION: arXiv:2204.01691v2; initial submission April 2022; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S039; DATE: 2026-08-23; WORK: Physical Agentic AI: An Architecture for Orchestrating a Robot Crew with LLMs; VERSION: arXiv:2608.22657v1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** Planning translated to software tools and language action translated to robot skills, with different physical assumptions.

**Development:** Retain selective reuse with explicit new obligations rather than either denying all transfer or assuming seamless equivalence.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain selective reuse with explicit new obligations rather than either denying all transfer or assuming seamless equivalence.; OPERATION: Identify which source assumptions survive translation and obtain new evidence for altered sensors, actuators, delays, failure costs and external participants.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Reuse unchanged logical components where their assumptions genuinely hold; do not recreate evidence for irrelevant differences.

**Problem:** Software tool success can be transferred rhetorically to robotics despite different observation, actuation, reversibility and timing.

**Mechanism:** Identify which source assumptions survive translation and obtain new evidence for altered sensors, actuators, delays, failure costs and external participants.

**Trigger and context:** Moving a mechanism between simulation, software interfaces and physical environments.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: A precise translation claim, known action/effect interfaces and protected target-specific tests.; PROPERTY_IDS: EAA-021; EAA-022; EAA-023; EAA-025; EAA-027; EAA-039; EAA-049; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Architecture integrator and evaluator

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Architecture integrator and evaluator; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. A precise translation claim, known action/effect interfaces and protected target-specific tests.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Helpful initiative remains delegated; untrusted content cannot legitimise new authority, and human handoff needs actual readiness. Consumer and prerequisite: Architecture integrator and evaluator. A precise translation claim, known action/effect interfaces and protected target-specific tests.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** The same architectural vocabulary does not erase new sensing, timing, authority or recovery obligations.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Reuse unchanged logical components where their assumptions genuinely hold; do not recreate evidence for irrelevant differences.

**Historical disposition:** STRONGLY_RETAINED

**Failure modes:** SayCan planning/execution gaps and simulation-only parts of 2026 evaluations show why demonstration context matters; a robot demonstration is not broad field assurance.; Introduced cost or harm: Validation expense, hardware risk and delayed deployment.

### EAA-067 — Coherent action basis across asynchronous state, goal and authority changes

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S022; DATE: 1999; WORK: Validating the DS1 Remote Agent Experiment; VERSION: ISAIRAS 1999 primary experiment report; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S016; DATE: 1987; WORK: An Investigation into Reactive Planning in Complex Domains; VERSION: AAAI 1987 pp.202–206; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S019; DATE: 1999; WORK: Principles of Mixed-Initiative User Interfaces; VERSION: CHI 1999; author manuscript; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S039; DATE: 2026-08-23; WORK: Physical Agentic AI: An Architecture for Orchestrating a Robot Crew with LLMs; VERSION: arXiv:2608.22657v1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** No single native original form: researcher-composed invariant from state freshness, commitments, scope and asynchronous execution.

**Development:** Retain a composition obligation with alternative implementations and explicit timing/observation limits.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain a composition obligation with alternative implementations and explicit timing/observation limits.; OPERATION: Ensure a consequential action is evaluated against a mutually compatible current-enough basis of relevant state, adopted goal and delegated scope; invalidate or recheck that basis after material concurrent changes.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A single atomic constrained operation or serial bounded controller may already ensure coherence; no universal transaction protocol is prescribed.

**Problem:** Each component may be locally correct while action uses an old state, a superseded goal and a newly invalid authority scope in combination.

**Mechanism:** Ensure a consequential action is evaluated against a mutually compatible current-enough basis of relevant state, adopted goal and delegated scope; invalidate or recheck that basis after material concurrent changes.

**Trigger and context:** Asynchronous observations, planners, human updates and in-flight actions share mutable decision context.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: Identifiable dependencies between state, goal, authority and action, plus an enforceable refresh/invalidation point.; PROPERTY_IDS: EAA-004; EAA-006; EAA-010; EAA-013; EAA-019; EAA-022; EAA-040; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Composition designer and executor

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Composition designer and executor; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Identifiable dependencies between state, goal, authority and action, plus an enforceable refresh/invalidation point.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Report material failure or unresolved effects; seek permission for genuine task changes and provide feasible handback. Consumer and prerequisite: Composition designer and executor. Identifiable dependencies between state, goal, authority and action, plus an enforceable refresh/invalidation point.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** An action cannot be justified by combining individually valid facts that never jointly governed the same decision.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** A single atomic constrained operation or serial bounded controller may already ensure coherence; no universal transaction protocol is prescribed.

**Historical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Failure modes:** This invariant is a researcher synthesis; DS1 demonstrates races and state mismatch but does not empirically validate every authority/goal formulation here.; Introduced cost or harm: Coordination, serialisation, cancelled work and difficulty observing distributed effects.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: This invariant is a researcher synthesis; DS1 demonstrates races and state mismatch but does not empirically validate every authority/goal formulation here.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C005; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S022; EAA-S039; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: This invariant is a researcher synthesis; DS1 demonstrates races and state mismatch but does not empirically validate every authority/goal formulation here.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S022; EAA-S039; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Only relevant dependencies need coherence; atomicity, acceptable staleness and concurrent change cannot be assumed from a diagram.

**Unresolved questions:** Could this action combine evidence, intent and permission from incompatible moments?; Only relevant dependencies need coherence; atomicity, acceptable staleness and concurrent change cannot be assumed from a diagram.

### EAA-068 — Failure-attributed revision rather than undirected self-change

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S022; DATE: 1999; WORK: Validating the DS1 Remote Agent Experiment; VERSION: ISAIRAS 1999 primary experiment report; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S031; DATE: 2023-10-10; WORK: Reflexion: Language Agents with Verbal Reinforcement Learning; VERSION: arXiv:2303.11366v4; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S025; DATE: 2022; WORK: Goal Misgeneralization in Deep Reinforcement Learning; VERSION: ICML 2022, PMLR 162, 12004–12019; final proceedings version; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S042; DATE: 2018; WORK: Recognising Assumption Violations in Autonomous Systems Verification; VERSION: AAMAS 2018 extended abstract pp.1933–1935; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** No single native original form: researcher-composed diagnostic loop from observed action, goal, oracle and model failures.

**Development:** Retain testable failure attribution and bounded revision, not a requirement to diagnose every failure completely before any safe response.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain testable failure attribution and bounded revision, not a requirement to diagnose every failure completely before any safe response.; OPERATION: Use discriminating observations to separate action failure, observation error, model mismatch, bad evaluation, goal ambiguity and inadequate capability; change only an authorised relevant mechanism and test the revised claim.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Apply a known local correction when the cause is established; otherwise preserve uncertainty and avoid broad self-modification.

**Problem:** A poor outcome can trigger changes to the wrong component, reinforce an oracle error or silently alter the intended goal.

**Mechanism:** Use discriminating observations to separate action failure, observation error, model mismatch, bad evaluation, goal ambiguity and inadequate capability; change only an authorised relevant mechanism and test the revised claim.

**Trigger and context:** Repeated failure or a proposed persistent change after an adverse outcome.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: Outcome evidence, candidate failure explanations, a bounded change surface and a meaningful subsequent test.; PROPERTY_IDS: EAA-005; EAA-023; EAA-024; EAA-034; EAA-045; EAA-050; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

<a id="eaa-069"></a>
### EAA-069 — Goal- and scope-preserving plan repair

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S006; DATE: 1988; WORK: Plans and Resource-Bounded Practical Reasoning; VERSION: Computational Intelligence 4, 349–355; 30-page author-formatted manuscript; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S019; DATE: 1999; WORK: Principles of Mixed-Initiative User Interfaces; VERSION: CHI 1999; author manuscript; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S039; DATE: 2026-08-23; WORK: Physical Agentic AI: An Architecture for Orchestrating a Robot Crew with LLMs; VERSION: arXiv:2608.22657v1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** No single comprehensive native form: practical goal continuity and delegated scope combined with a recent request-preserving action gate.

**Development:** Retain explicit separation of plan revision from goal/authority revision, with user negotiation when task meaning changes.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain explicit separation of plan revision from goal/authority revision, with user negotiation when task meaning changes.; OPERATION: Allow alternatives in means only within the current task and authority; treat changes to the intended goal, named entities or protected constraints as a separate decision requiring appropriate authorisation.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A pre-authorised equivalent alternative can be selected directly; a harmless change of means need not require a new conversation.

**Problem:** A repair can make a plan executable by silently changing the destination, task object or protected requirement.

**Mechanism:** Allow alternatives in means only within the current task and authority; treat changes to the intended goal, named entities or protected constraints as a separate decision requiring appropriate authorisation.

**Trigger and context:** Failed plans, unavailable destinations/tools or a generated substitute that would improve a success score.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: Identifiable invariant task conditions, permissible degrees of freedom and a route to negotiate genuine goal changes.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Planner, executor and delegator

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Planner, executor and delegator; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Identifiable invariant task conditions, permissible degrees of freedom and a route to negotiate genuine goal changes.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Report material failure or unresolved effects; seek permission for genuine task changes and provide feasible handback. Consumer and prerequisite: Planner, executor and delegator. Identifiable invariant task conditions, permissible degrees of freedom and a route to negotiate genuine goal changes.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** Repaired execution still pursues the authorised request, or the changed task is explicitly accepted rather than hidden as success.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** A pre-authorised equivalent alternative can be selected directly; a harmless change of means need not require a new conversation.

**Historical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Failure modes:** Equivalence of means can be ambiguous; rigid literal preservation can reject a genuinely desired alternative. The 2026 preprint’s unrecoverable faults test refusal, not general repair quality.; Introduced cost or harm: Blocked useful substitutions and clarification latency.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Equivalence of means can be ambiguous; rigid literal preservation can reject a genuinely desired alternative. The 2026 preprint’s unrecoverable faults test refusal, not general repair quality.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C006; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S033; EAA-S039; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Equivalence of means can be ambiguous; rigid literal preservation can reject a genuinely desired alternative. The 2026 preprint’s unrecoverable faults test refusal, not general repair quality.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S033; EAA-S039; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Goal equivalence and delegator intent need task-specific evidence; the invariant is broader analytical synthesis than one paper’s contract gate.

**Unresolved questions:** Did this repair change only the means, or did it quietly change what was requested or permitted?; Goal equivalence and delegator intent need task-specific evidence; the invariant is broader analytical synthesis than one paper’s contract gate.

Simplification is acceptable only while the cheaper path still establishes the relevant criterion.; OMISSION_OR_RETIREMENT_CONDITIONS: Omit or retire a dedicated implementation when this trigger no longer applies, its consumer disappears, or an existing cheaper mechanism demonstrably establishes the same criterion. <a id="eaa-070"></a>
### EAA-070 — Predecessor-criterion evaluation of self-modification

**Historical origin:** ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S053; DATE: 2016; WORK: Self-Modification of Policy and Utility Function in Rational Agents; VERSION: AGI 2016 conference version, 10 pages; DOI 10.1007/978-3-319-41649-6_1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.

**Original form:** A formal realistic value function evaluates anticipated policy changes under the predecessor utility rather than a successor’s rewritten utility.

**Development:** Retain a narrow formal guard against successor self-certification, not general warrant for autonomous self-revision.

**Evolved form at historical freeze:** FORM: Retain a narrow formal guard against successor self-certification, not general warrant for autonomous self-revision.; OPERATION: In the specified formal model, evaluate anticipated post-change policy consequences using the predecessor utility and modification-independent beliefs, rather than letting a successor utility certify itself.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use a fixed mechanism or externally reviewed bounded update when broad self-modification is unnecessary or its assumptions cannot be established.

**Problem:** Evaluating a change by the changed utility or ignoring its future policy effects can make self-modification appear beneficial for the wrong reason.

**Mechanism:** In the specified formal model, evaluate anticipated post-change policy consequences using the predecessor utility and modification-independent beliefs, rather than letting a successor utility certify itself.

**Trigger and context:** Explicit policy or utility self-modification under a model capable of representing its future effects.

**Preconditions:** CONDITIONS: The model’s optimality/anticipation and modification-independence conditions, plus a justified initial criterion.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

**Decision or consumer:** Formal agent designer and authorised revision reviewer

**Authority boundary:** ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Formal agent designer and authorised revision reviewer; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. The model’s optimality/anticipation and modification-independence conditions, plus a justified initial criterion.

**Communication or interaction:** FUNCTION: Human correction is informative but fallible; persistent memory also raises privacy and consent questions. Consumer and prerequisite: Formal agent designer and authorised revision reviewer. The model’s optimality/anticipation and modification-independence conditions, plus a justified initial criterion.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

**Observable criterion:** The theorem’s predecessor-utility value is preserved on reachable histories under its assumptions; no broader safety or corrigibility result is asserted.

**Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path:** Use a fixed mechanism or externally reviewed bounded update when broad self-modification is unnecessary or its assumptions cannot be established.

**Historical disposition:** ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE

**Failure modes:** Preserving an initially wrong utility is not acceptable adaptation; the paper explicitly separates tampering and warns that this stability can obstruct value learning, exploration or corrigibility.; Introduced cost or harm: Prediction/evaluation expense, rigidity and possible preservation of harmful objectives.

**Criticism:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Preserving an initially wrong utility is not acceptable adaptation; the paper explicitly separates tampering and warns that this stability can obstruct value learning, exploration or corrigibility.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C008; EAA-C013; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S053; EAA-S025; EAA-S041; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.

**Contrary evidence:** STATEMENT: Preserving an initially wrong utility is not acceptable adaptation; the paper explicitly separates tampering and warns that this stability can obstruct value learning, exploration or corrigibility.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S053; EAA-S025; EAA-S041; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.

**Assumption sensitivity:** Ideal rationality and accurate anticipation are strong; practical approximation and target validity remain unproved for open-ended deployed agents.

**Unresolved questions:** By which pre-existing criterion is the change judged, and is preserving that criterion itself justified?; Ideal rationality and accurate anticipation are strong; practical approximation and target validity remain unproved for open-ended deployed agents.
